> **BRAND-COLORS-BACKFILL-001 + BRAND-COLORS-PERSIST-001 (data leg) — DONE (2026-07-29 desktop session, Opus 4.8; cloud data patched live + code already on `main` via `a1c8f6c`/`250f504`; no new deploy owed).** Owner: "upgrade old batch, make sure nothing is missing: brand colors, sections, slogan." **Census of all 34 tracked applications (`GET /api/applications` per artifact id):** every row already carried FULL sections (15 CV + 9 CL) AND a slogan — nothing missing there. Verified live: demant `#24405b`, NKT `#720002`, NVIDIA `#3b5c00`, Terma `#956f00`, etc. **4 `brandfit=Y` rows SKIPPED on purpose** (dtu-wind/hays/ibsen/lightera): their capture returned only the default `#1d2b45` fallback (no real sample — DTU's brand is red, Hays' green), so pinning a generic navy would look branded without being the brand — left on the user's default style. 1 (everllence) app already deleted. The app applies these on Open via `meta.styleConfig` (BRAND-FIT-OPEN-001), independent of the island `brandSc`. **Code (already landed on `main`, this session's commits, carried up by a concurrent rebase):** `BRAND-COLORS-PERSIST-001` — `gen-runner.py` now projects `slots`→`meta.styleConfig` GATED on the row's `brandfit` toggle (a default `#1d2b45` palette never reaches styleConfig); the island `brandSc` now reads BOTH shapes (prefer `slots`, fall back to flat `navy/accent`, reject the `#1d2b45` default). Landed as `a1c8f6c` (island + rebuilt `antcv-react-islands.js`) + the gen-runner change + `250f504` (CL load-from-history fidelity). **PR #357** (`preserve/brand-colors-persist-cl-fidelity`) **CLOSED as redundant** — all its substance was already on `main`; the only remaining diff was a conflicting cache-bust bump. **PROCESS NOTE for the next session:** the shared desktop clone was under heavy concurrent use — a parallel lane `git reset --hard origin/main` mid-work and churned versions 3942→3982 every few minutes, wiping a staged merge and moving a local branch; the conflict resolution had to be done in an isolated `git worktree` (with a PowerShell `New-Item -ItemType Junction` node_modules link, since Git-Bash `mklink` malforms the path). Prefer a worktree for any sustained work while nightly routines are active.

Tests: `pwa/antcv-jobtracker-doc-nonstring.test.mjs` (11; the behavioural half runs the REAL `api.ts` via `ts.transpileModule` and SKIPS cleanly when `typescript` is not installed). **Forensics recipe:** D1 `ant_memory` tells you where a flow died without a repro — no new `application` row ⇒ it exited before `createApplication`; `artifacts[].generated_at` in **seconds** = gen-runner wrote it, **ms** = the island. > **HDR-TYPE-CONTROLS-001 — DONE / SHIPPED (2026-07-29 desktop session, Opus 5; PWA `1.51.3862-hdr-type-ctrl`, docx-worker `1.14.173-hdr-type-ctrl` DEPLOYED (run 30450517787, `/health`-confirmed)).** Owner: the panel must control SIZE **and** compression/expansion (letter spacing, 0.05 steps, signed) for **Name, Specialisation, Application, Contact line and Slogan**, in all modes, on the CV **and** the CL, in preview **and** export — "and make sure nothing prevents the user from controlling these values"; Specialisation and Application must be SEPARATE controls (they are no longer in the same place) and the Slogan needs one at all. **Shipped.** New `fontSizes` keys: `applicationSize`, `sloganSize` (sizes) + `nameTrack`, `specTrack`, `applicationTrack`, `contactTrack`, `sloganTrack` (letter-spacing DELTAS in pt). 0.05pt = exactly one twentieth of a point = the DOCX `w:spacing` unit, so a panel step maps onto Word character spacing losslessly; DELTA semantics mean 0 = unchanged, so every existing document renders byte-identically. Panel: `Specialisation / Application` split into two rows, `Slogan` row added, a signed ±0.05 letter-spacing control on each of the five identity rows, plus an `All Identity Lines ↔` bulk row. **The "nothing prevents the user" sweep found four real blockers, all fixed:** (a) `antcv-docx-client.js` `buildFontSizes()` is a WHITELIST and dropped all seven new keys — every control would have been silently inert on the DOCX/PDF leg; (b) the copenhagen measured fit emits `letter-spacing … !important` for the name with no panel escape — the panel deltas are now emitted AFTER the fit rules so the owner is the last word; (c) the docx-worker hard-pinned the CL slogan at 11pt/`characterSpacing` 20 and the application line at 10.5pt/4 — both read the payload now, and `__cphNameFit` folds the name delta into its fitted track; (d) `antcv-pdf-preview-gate.js`'s fallback export path read `personalInfo.fontSizes` (a legacy mirror that is usually absent) instead of the canonical `localStorage.fontSizes`, so that path shipped with NO owner sizing. **Bug found on the way — APPLINE-PARITY-001:** the CL application line was **11px in the preview and 10.5pt (14px) in the export**, a 27% preview/export split; both legs are 10.5pt now and read the same key (the slogan's 15px-vs-11pt split is likewise closed at 11pt). Wired across 30 sites in `pwa/app.js` (prelude helpers on `window`, defaults ×3, tracking stepper, panel, live preview band, live preview CL slogan + app line, the `name_block`/`spec_block`/`contact_line` figure-layout leg preview + HTML, HTML export band, HTML export CL, in-app DOCX stylesheet `<w:spacing>`), the copenhagen sidecar, the docx client, the pdf-preview gate and the docx-worker. **Verified:** PWA suite **1504/1504**, workers **290/290**, render V&V **50/50** docx diags incl. the new `diag-hdr-type-controls.mjs` (12 checks — every key reaches its run, each track shifts `w:spacing` by exactly pt×20, and an all-zero payload is byte-identical to no payload); `node --check` clean; cache-bust quintet applied and asserted by test (STALE_VERSIONS invariant respected). **MIRRORED into `pwa/app.src.js`** (31 sites; corrects an earlier call in this session not to). The file is NOT stale — a marker census shows every one of the 68 ticket markers in `app.js` is present there, i.e. sessions have kept it covered; only THIS change was missing. Its short names come from an older minifier pass (fontSizes is `Yr` there, `ca` in `app.js`), so mirroring is by MEANING, by hand — not mechanical. **Regenerating it from `app.js` was tried and rejected:** a prettier pass gated on AST equivalence produced a provably identical program, but the mirror is the SOLE home of ~376 ticket-marked design rationales that minification strips (444 markers in `app.src.js` vs 68 in `app.js`), and it broke 74 assertions across 43 test files that anchor on those comments. New mirror-lock tests now pin key-count parity and assert the rationale set may never shrink. **Deployed and live-verified:** PWA `app.js?v=1.51.3862-hdr-type-ctrl` + `sw.js` CACHE served from antcv.pages.dev with `__antcvTrkStep` present in the deployed bundle; docx-worker `/health` = `1.14.173-hdr-type-ctrl` (dispatch run 30450517787, PWA push run first, one-deployer rule respected). Detail: `docs/qa/SESSION_LOG_2026-07-29_HDR_TYPE_CONTROLS.md`.

Fixed the fixture, so the check now has teeth. (b) TWO OOXML COLOUR SYNTAXES: a RUN colour is `<w:color w:val="X"/>` while a BORDER colour is inline `w:color="X"`. > **PREVIEW-PAGINATION-DIAGS-STALE-001 - DONE (2026-07-26, render-capable desktop session; test-only, NO app/worker source touched; pwa suite 1491 pass / 0 fail; nightly: do NOT re-pick).** The two remaining RED preview-pagination diags were both STALE MECHANISM, not app bugs - each was measured before being changed. (1) `diag-experience-npage` reported "experience map stable (no oscillation)" as FAILING. It snapped the map once at a fixed 11s and compared with a single re-read 4s later. A 35s probe showed the greedy multi-page walk is still CONVERGING at 11s and reaches a FIXED POINT at ~13.5s which then holds unchanged for 30+ seconds - a slow settle raced by a magic number, not a storm. The diag now POLLS until two consecutive reads agree (bounded ~30s), then asserts it STAYS agreed, so it tests the real intent and is immune to host speed. IMPORTANT SIDE-FINDING: the same probe run against the PRE-SALMON bundles showed the map churning far worse (whole map remapped AND pageRows 3->5 between reads), so SALMON-BREAK-SITE-001 measurably IMPROVED preview convergence rather than causing this. (2) `diag-export-preview-pages` reported TWO failures ("2 pager chips" and "chip 2 scrolls to page 2") from ONE stale selector: it counted every `button` in the pager, which now includes the non-numeric "Legacy ATS view" toggle added after the diag was written - and because the scroll block was gated on `chips.length === 2`, the extra button silently SKIPPED the scroll test, which then reported false. Now filtered to numeric page chips for both the count and the click. The scroll itself was never broken - it passes as soon as it is actually exercised. Lesson for future triage: a gated sub-check can turn one stale selector into a fake second failure. ALSO in this pass: `docs/design/COPENHAGEN_MODERN_NORDIC_PALETTE_SPEC.md` Status was corrected - it still listed all EIGHT render flags as OPEN after CPH-RENDER-FLAGS-001 shipped them (1.51.3822/3823 + wk 1.14.172), which would have sent a future session to redo shipped work; it now records what shipped, the copenhagen gating, and the roles-richblock-adapter gotcha.**

(1) **CV-3P**: the page governor had only DELETE levers, wrong as a repair for a SAVED app (owner rule: compress, never wholesale-delete). NEW `scripts/job-tracker/cv_fit.py` - byte-exact render -> measure_density -> targeted rewrite -> re-render, acceptance is the measured page count only; candidates rank by RELATIVE cost so gentle blocks pay first; a rewrite that frees no rendered LINE is refused as churn; source located by similarity (expTense rewrites verbs at payload time, so the rendered bullet is never byte-equal to the stored one); gates include two found on live output - NO ABBREVIATION and NO INVENTION. The sweep is now DISPOSABLE-ONLY - it may delete a row with NO generated content (the stub a crashed gen leaves), never one carrying a CV or CL; over-cap is reported as `history_over_cap` so pruning is deliberate; the collection LIST went 50 -> 200 (keeping a row then hiding it is the same bug in a hat). (3) **FOREIGN-NIGHT-WRITER**: every POST creating a SECTIONLESS row now logs its caller (device_id, UA, origin, referer, sec-fetch-site, cf-ray, country) so the next occurrence names itself, and only a BROWSER-shaped caller may move the global active pointer - a headless caller has no screen to update. This stops the damage at the source, including when the script dies before it can restore the pointer. (4) **CPH-RENDER-FLAGS-001**: all eight open mockup-divergence flags in one pass, gated on copenhagen-modern so every other package renders byte-identically - 1.5pt rule sweep (export mirror 8 -> 12 eighths), grey #777777 section-head underlines (main only), per-role rule dropped, Results lead-in upright + grey underline, {grp} sub-head rules + right-set years #777, body links #0B4F8A, sidebar radius 9px. > **RELAY-HEALTH-1042-ATTEST-DEFER-001 — OBSERVATION, report-only (2026-07-28 CI nightly, Opus 4.8; NOT a regression, NOT filed as a bug).** During the nightly live-attest, the access-relay `/health` and `/api/health` endpoints returned Cloudflare **error 1042** on 3 retries from the Actions runner, so the running `RELAY_VERSION` string could not be read this run. Root-caused as an **endpoint-level upstream-probe failure, not a worker outage**: `/health` "probes upstream to report real provider key state" (`workers/access-relay/src/index.js:12`) — a worker→upstream subrequest that 1042'd tonight — while a plain `GET /` to the SAME host returned a clean **404** (the worker executed and routed → it is live). No regression signal: the relay source is unchanged on main since 07-24 (`RELAY_VERSION = 'auth-36-jd-cross-app-guard'`), and no worker-`src` reached main since the 07-26 CI base. The other three workers (cv-proxy/demo-proxy `3.8.4-brand-ink-match`, docx-worker `1.14.171-spec-photo`) + the PWA (`1.51.3803-word-sheet`) all attested live and matched in-repo source. Filed so a future sweep seeing 1042 recognises it as a **known transient version-attest gap** (worker up, source pinned), not a new fault. If `/health` 1042s persistently across multiple nights AND `GET /` also stops returning 404, THEN escalate — that would be a real relay outage. Owed: a run where the relay `/health` upstream probe resolves (desktop, or a later CI run) to confirm the live `auth-36` version. See `docs/qa/NIGHTLY_2026-07-28_CI_REPORT.md`. **UPDATE 2026-07-29 (CI nightly, Opus 4.8): RECURRED identically — 2nd consecutive night.** `/health` 1042 on retry; `GET /` to the same host returns **404** (worker live, routed); relay source still `auth-36-jd-cross-app-guard`, unchanged on main. Escalation threshold NOT met — the escalation condition is `/health` 1042 AND `GET /` no longer 404; the root 404 confirms the worker is up, so this remains a known transient version-attest gap, not an outage. > **DIAG-SALMON-EMPTY-REGION-STALE-001 — FILED, report-only (2026-07-26 CI nightly, Opus 4.8; render-domain; NOT a regression; NOT fixed — salmon = most blue-screen-prone per CLAUDE.md).** The standalone diag `pwa/test/diag-salmon-empty-region.mjs` (last touched 2026-06-20 `1.50.753`; not in CI / not in `run-tests.mjs`, so it gates nothing) FAILS on current main `1.51.3803`. Diagnostic-first Playwright probe root-caused it as a **stale fixture + obsolete assertions, not a live pagination bug**: (a) the 5-role fixture's main content = **1304px** vs the CURRENT usable preview page height **1275px** → `antcv:mainOverflow` verdict **"squeeze"** (overshoot 29px / 1.8 lines) so the engine correctly keeps ONE page (`pageRows=1`) — the diag was calibrated for the old ~1053px usable page where 1304px was clearly 2 pages, and the usable height has since grown, dropping the fixture into the small-overshoot single-page band; (b) its "LAST page-row = full A4 1123px" assertion is obsolete twice — `antcv-page-fit.js` now collapses even the last multi-page row to content (`rows.length===1 ? sheetHeightPx() : '0px'`) AND PREVIEW-SHEET-WORD-HEIGHT-001 (`1.51.3803`) made the single/last sheet the Word-equivalent ~985px (=1123/1.14), not true A4. No corroborating regression: in-CI salmon/storm guards green (suite 1482/1482), copenhagen-storm + sidebar + settings + button diags all PASS, all workers attest live. **Owed to a render-capable/desktop session** (the diag asserts preview↔Word-export parity that needs a real render to confirm): bump the fixture past the squeeze band (≈7–8 roles), drop the obsolete last-row-A4 assert and assert every page-row collapses to its content (the surviving SALMON-EMPTY-REGION invariant), keep the flush-salmon-gap check, then optionally wire into `run-tests.mjs`. Same class as DOCX-DIAG-STALE-OR-REGRESSED-001. See OPEN_REGISTER + `docs/qa/NIGHTLY_2026-07-26_CI_REPORT.md`. **UPDATE 2026-07-26 (desktop nightly, Opus 4.8): the "bump to 7–8 roles" recipe is INSUFFICIENT — attempted, reverted the WIP, still OPEN.** The headless harness does not paginate at all: the preview MEASUREMENT pass that populates `antcv:autoPagesPreview` / sets the page count `u` does not run to completion in the seeded `step:'editor'` context within the wait window, so even ~2× a page (measured 2015px) stays `pageRows=1` (`u=Math.max(1,...pages)` defaults to 1). Two further fixture bugs found: (a) the fixture `profile` renders as an empty "(click to add)" placeholder — the current build no longer reads its `text` field, so page 1 is near-empty; (b) forcing the split via manual `role.page` (which DOES win over the measurer in the render role→page map `d`) exposes the measurer partially running and reordering roles across 3 page-boxes. A clean, stable 2-page render is not reliably reproducible headlessly — proper repair needs the in-app Browser pane driving the real Preview tab + awaiting the measurer (or a fully deterministic seeded `autoPagesPreview` map + the profile-field fix). Filed not shipped (a fragile half-working test would violate "an end result, not a brickable mid product").

> **JOBTRACKER-EMIT-SCHEMA-TRUNCATE-001 — FIXED (2026-07-25 job-tracker nightly, worktree-isolated).** `build_workbook.py`'s "emit job_tracker_doc.json" step (runs as the `JOB_BUILD` subprocess on EVERY `pull --render`, AFTER `job-tracker-sync.py` writes the full cloud doc to the same path) rebuilt `_doc_out` as a bare literal `{"version","envelope","rows","urls","support","artifacts","jd"}` — 7 keys — silently DROPPING `brand`/`brandfit`/`queue`/`signals`/`webintel`/`discovered`/`gen`/`pin`/`park` from the on-disk `job_tracker_doc.json` every single render, even though the script already had the full doc in `_DOC` (loaded from `JOB_DOC` at the top of the file) and already special-cased preserving `artifacts`/`jd` individually — the newer schema-v2 keys were just never added to that preserve-list as the relay schema grew. **Impact (latent, not yet triggered): a `push` or `import-xlsx` run AFTER any `pull --render` would PUT this truncated local doc straight to the cloud** — `cmd_push`/`cmd_import_xlsx` in `job-tracker-sync.py` load whatever is on disk and PUT it verbatim (the relay does a rev-checked full-doc overwrite, no server-side per-key merge) — wiping all tracker brand/queue/signal/discovery data cloud-side. Found while investigating an owner report that recent generations were missing brand colours (the app-side brandV2 data was confirmed FINE — see the DONE entry below — but the local doc audit that followed surfaced this separate, more serious latent bug). Verified: direct `GET /api/job-tracker` shows the true cloud doc has always carried all 16 keys (rev 153, e.g. `queue` had 47 entries, `brand` 35, `signals` 9) — this was ONLY a local-mirror bug, the cloud data itself was never at risk from reads, only from a future write. **Fix** (`build_workbook.py`, owner's Drive file, not in the repo): `_doc_out=dict(_DOC) if _DOC else {}` then `.update({...the 7 script-managed keys...})` — preserves every other key verbatim, only overrides what this script actually derives. **Verified**: re-ran `pull --render` post-fix — local doc keys now `['artifacts','brand','brandfit','discovered','envelope','gen','jd','park','pin','queue','rows','signals','support','urls','version','webintel']` (all 16), `brand`=35/`queue`=47/`signals`=9 entries intact, Excel + Proposed Inbox tab rebuilt clean (rev 154). No push was run tonight (no generation this run, so no doc changes were owed) — this closes the exposure before it could ever fire. **Owner note on the brand-colours report:** apps 2750 (Hamamatsu) + 2751 (Terma) — this nightly's own 2026-07-24 output — both carry `meta.brandV2` with real researched colours server-side; this matches the BRAND-EVERYWHERE + PAGEMAP-PER-APP-001 fix that shipped later the same day (PWA `1.51.3763-pagemap-per-app`), which found a client-side meta-clobber as the actual cause of "brand colours not applying" — a rendering/stale-build issue, not missing data. If colours are still missing after a Hard Refresh on a current build, that's a new instance worth a fresh report with the specific app.

Known cost, deliberate: a preview page-box may show bottom whitespace where Word's ~14% taller content fills the sheet - the honest break site wins; if the whitespace bothers, the follow-up is shrinking the CV page-box height to the Word-equivalent line, never moving the break. > **CPH-HEADER-BAND-OVERFLOW-STORM-001 — OPEN (2026-07-23, CI nightly / GitHub Actions, Opus 4.8; verified headless, filed not fixed).** The Copenhagen Stage-3 header band (DEFAULT ON; kill `antcv:copenhagen-v2=0`) drives a RUNAWAY layout-growth loop in the CV preview. On any CV, the single page-row's usable height climbs ~15px on EVERY measure cycle without bound (`antcv:mainOverflow.usablePx` 1053 → 1608+ over 9s; `totalMainPx`/`rows`/verdict constant) — so `antcv-main-overflow-detect-364.js` re-writes `localStorage['antcv:mainOverflow']` ~4×/s FOREVER and the preview (`.antcv-document-sidebar` + `.antcv-document-main` + `.antcv-preview-paper`) reflows continuously = permanent main-thread churn (the PERF-001 class). 364's osc-damp only catches a two-state A,B,A,B flip; a monotone climb slips past it. **Root cause (grower):** the `antcv-sidebar-fill-equalize-227` / `antcv-page-fit` chain — with the band present, the equalize LAST/single-row branch extends the sidebar to `mainH`, the row grows to the taller column, `main` (align-self:stretch) grows to the new row height, so `mainH` drifts UP each cycle and the idempotency guard (`data-antcv-eq-h === String(mainH)`) never holds. The band's `display:grid;align-content:center;row-gap:18px` (buildCSS ~160) is what makes the row-height non-idempotent under equalize. **Bisect (headless):** CONVERGED = 1 write, 0px drift at `1.51.1972`/`c77e38f2`; RUNAWAY = 35 writes at `c336c5a5` (Copenhagen Stage-2b table + Stage-3 header band, `1.51.3061` + wk 1.14.163) — the introducing commit. Kill switch `antcv:copenhagen-v2=0` restores convergence (1 write, 0px drift) on HEAD → verified both paths. **Detected by** `diag-settings-panels-probe.mjs` (row 17): DIAG FAIL on `antcv:mainOverflow` set-count 21-23×/6s (DOM mutations 0 — it is a localStorage/reflow storm, not a DOM churn), where 07-22 it passed (07-22 base = 1972, pre-band). **Impact:** every user on the default template (Copenhagen is default-ON since 3061) pays continuous forced-reflow while the CV preview is open. **Not fixed here (deliberate):** the fix is in the equalize/page-fit/band interaction — CLAUDE.md flags this (salmon/pagination) as the most blue-screen-prone area, and a preview-layout change cannot be live-verified from CI (no Browser pane) nor safely proven against real multi-page + salmon cases with a headless single-page fixture. Filed with a permanent repro `pwa/test/diag-copenhagen-overflow-storm.mjs` (PASS = ≤6 writes AND ≤8px drift with the band ON *and* OFF; currently FAILs ON / PASSes OFF). **Fix next (render-capable/desktop session):** make the equalize target the CONTENT height not the stretched `mainH` in the single-row+band case, OR give 364's damp a monotone-climb detector (freeze when the last N signatures differ only by a steadily-growing `usablePx`), OR make the band grid not feed its own container's height back into the equalize measure. Owner workaround today: `localStorage['antcv:copenhagen-v2']='0'`. Report: `docs/qa/NIGHTLY_2026-07-23_CI_REPORT.md`.

Root cause: `antcv-quick-doc-color.js` (QUICK-COLOR-RETARGET-001, 1.51.3222) injected `.antcv-preview-paper table tr:first-child th, table tr:first-child td { background:<hex> !important; color:#FFF !important }` — `tr:first-child` matches the first tr of ITS OWN parent, so it hit the thead row (intended) AND the first TBODY row (its td cells), whose `!important` beat the inline band. Only visible with a quick doc colour picked (`antcv:quickDocColor`); with navy picked the header looked unchanged, masking the source. Fix: selector scoped to `table thead th / thead td` (the app's only table header shape — single thead site in app.src.js). Sidecar-only, no app.js edit; export unaffected (worker `tableHeaderBg` shades the header row only). Fixture-verified in-browser through the real sidecar: header navy/white, rows band #DCE5EA/#fff/#DCE5EA/#fff. Ships as the sidecar `?v=1.51.3502-corecomp-row` (index.html); the GLOBAL version tokens (seed/SW CACHE/version-override TARGET) stay at the parallel lane's newer `1.51.3522-fit-stable` — JS is network-first (F5-FRESH-001) so the unique `?v` alone propagates the fix.

> **SECTIONS-STORM-2026-07-23 — ALL DONE / live-verified (Fable 5; PWA `1.51.3482-sections-storm`; suite 1464 green; nightly: do NOT re-pick).** Owner console probe (DTU Wind, 1.51.3342): `sections` rewritten CONTINUOUSLY through the whole sidecar chain; U-toggle (lead-in underline) reverting in msec; Results on one role only; exports on a stale dark palette. ONE root, three legs. **(1) STORM ENGINE (root):** BOTH app.js sections-ingest sites (initial state + the `antcv:sections-updated` re-ingest handler) rebuilt the blob as a fresh `{cv,cl}`, dropping the belts' STAMP-IN-BLOB root fields (`_roleMergeStamp`/`_sidebarCutStamp`); the 500ms auto-save wrote the stampless blob back → role-merge-stored + sidebar-relevance-cut re-armed EVERY cycle → write+dispatch → re-ingest → forever. Fix: spread the parsed blob (`{...parsed, cv, cl}`) at both sites (app.src.js + minified app.js), AND substructure-key the stamps per `storm-guards-must-be-substructure-keyed` — role-merge stamps the EXPERIENCE section (`exp._roleMergeStamp`), sidebar-cut stamps each cut-eligible section (`sec._cutStamp` on tools/certs/regulatory); either site matching stands the belt down, a restored pre-cut snapshot (no stamps anywhere) still re-arms. Live-verified on the patched bundle: seeded DTU-Wind-shaped state → 8 one-shot writes, then ZERO writes over 20s of poller cycles; stamps intact after the app.js auto-save. The U-toggle revert was this storm clobbering un-persisted React state inside the 500ms autosave window (the section-patch path itself is a spread-merge and preserves `leadUnderline`) — dead with the storm. **(2) RULE-17 RESULTS (">1 Result"):** `mergeSameCompanyRoles` (antcv-docx-client) built the merged role as `{...grp[0]}` — only the FIRST constituent's `results` survived; now unions the distinct non-empty results in constituent order (export+stored parity, role-merge-stored reuses the same fn). Plus RESULTS-HEAL in role-merge-stored: stored merged roles with COLLAPSED results (empty or exactly one constituent's line) are repaired from their hidden constituents even on a stamped blob; any other text = owner edit, sticks. Live-verified: merged Innoviz role carries BOTH results. **(3) EXPORT-PALETTE-PARITY:** the PACKAGE-PALETTE-MIX-001 self-heal fixed React state ONLY — `localStorage.styleConfig` kept the stale palette and docx-client `buildStyle` exports FROM it. The heal now PERSISTS (`u.set('styleConfig')` + cloud `Qn`), merging over CURRENT state so non-palette prefs (expTense, indents…) survive; live-verified: seeded the owner's dark theme → reload → styleConfig = copenhagen 1.51.3101 tokens (#00746E/#777777/#01B9BD/#DCE5EA/ring 1.5), expTense preserved, package NOT flipped to custom. Tests: role-merge-stored 12 (substamp + heal + union), sidebar-relevance-cut 15 (substamp holds / pre-cut re-arms), NEW `merged-results-union.test.mjs` (real docx-client fn) + `sections-root-keys-preserved.test.mjs` (structural spread-ingest + heal-persist invariants, both bundles). Cache-bust quintet complete (app.js + docx-client + both sidecars ?v, sw.js CACHE, TARGET_VERSION + STALE add `1.51.3442-gap14`, ANTCV_VERSION seed).

> **KERNEL-DATA-DRIFT-001 — near-regression AVOIDED / procedure (2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; data-only, NOT a code bug).** Merging a new Gabriel `personalInfo` JSON (Downloads `..._2026-07-17_v10`) into the canonical revealed the on-disk file lineage (v11/v10) had drifted BEHIND the LIVE cloud kernel: the cloud already had the Project-management tool rows, 8 `stylePrefs.semanticConstraintsV2`, 3 `writingPrefs.chips`, the `•`-marker unsolicited `specialization`, the long `workStyle.work_style_line_en`, the richer MISWG/DDIS `eligibility.securityClearance`, PLUS cloud-only keys NO file has (`__latin_*`/`__zh_*` babel variants, top-level `bannedWords`/`bannedPhrases`, `foundation`, `recommendations`, `subtitle`, 15 `additional` vs the file's 10, `visibilityControls.showPhotoByDefault`). A blind file import would have REGRESSED all of it. **Correct procedure (used): append additively onto the LIVE kernel — do NOT import a whole file.** Patch only genuinely-new fields (structured `eligibility`, background/accessibility "not limited his career" sentence, rugby `INTEREST-RUGBY-LITERAL-001` override pin, `migrationVersion`→`2026-07-17-modernized-v12`), back up `personalInfo` to a timestamped localStorage key FIRST, then push via the app's own `window.AntcvGenerateCloudSync277.syncBothWays()` (PUT-then-GET). The programmatic cloud PUT is **classifier-gated** (mutates a real account) → owner runs the final one-liner (or clicks Generate, same sync). Canonical superset saved to OneDrive `..._2026-07-17_v12.json`; repo snapshot Eligibility section mirrored (no contact PII). Lesson: for Gabriel, LIVE cloud > any on-disk file — reconcile FROM cloud, never blind-import.

> **AUTOSAVE-STALE-CLOBBER-001 — DONE (2026-07-22, PWA `1.51.3040-app-rev-guard` + relay `auth-35-autosave-rev-guard`, both deployed; nightly: do not re-pick).** Server-rev guard on auto-save, jobTracker-rev pattern: relay PUT `/api/applications/:id` accepts optional `base_rev` (= the `updated_at` the client last loaded) and rejects a mismatch **409 conflict with the row untouched**; absent/null keeps the unconditional write (Python nightly / partial PUTs / old clients unchanged). Client `antcv-app-rev-guard.js` (request-only fetch-guard, no app.js edits): per-TAB in-memory rev stamps from every GET/PUT response (not localStorage — a stale sibling tab cannot borrow a fresh stamp), injects `base_rev` into every PUT, converts a 409 into a logged synthetic 200 skip, and does NOT advance the stamp on conflict so a stale tab stays blocked until it re-loads the row. Kill switch `antcv:disable-app-rev-guard`. Relay diag 6/6 + downgrade-guard 5/5; browser harness verified the stamp→inject→409-skip→no-advance→re-stamp cycle; suite 1444/1444.

> **CL-APP-SUBTITLE-HEADING-SWAP-001 SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2541` 4c639b7).** Owner: the cover letter showed the application line TWICE — inside the heading (wrong) + under the slogan (correct). Owner also noted "a bit of jumpiness" in the CL header — the application-line sidecar re-injects its node during the header's text-align churn (it already has anti-storm measures, DEBOUNCE-STARVATION-001); removing the heading duplicate reduces conflicting renders, but if it persists the sidecar's re-inject cadence is the place to look.

> **HEADER-APP-LINE-001 CORRECTED per owner (1.51.2520, `antcv-application-line-001.js`; live-verified).** Owner refined: the CV must NOT carry the application line; the COVER LETTER has the application line OUT of the heading (under the slogan), and the CL heading shows the SPECIALIZATION where it used to render "Application: [role]". Sidecar now: (1) `isCL()` gate → the line + swap run on the CL ONLY (CV reverts to name/specialization/contact, no line — verified `CV_appLinePresent:false`); (2) `swapHeadingToSpec()` rewrites the CL heading leaf whose text is "Application: [role]" to `personalInfo.specialization` (role-matched; live-verified "Application: Senior Project Manager…" → "Processes • Products • People"); (3) the "Application for [role] at [company]" line stays under the slogan (contact-band fallback when no CL slogan). React reverts the heading only on real re-renders (0 in ~2s idle); the 1.5s interval re-applies. Still OPEN: export parity (docx-worker header). Cache-bust 2500→2520 (churn).

> **JD-FETCH SuccessFactors SPA — FIXED + LIVE-VERIFIED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; deployed cv-proxy + antcv-demo-proxy from `015ca30`).** Owner: pasting a SAP SuccessFactors job URL (`career5.successfactors.eu`, Terma req 1244) loaded no JD — the app kept the previous 3Shape JD and generated 3Shape (this is the true root of the "Terma → 3Shape" report, NOT a meta-stick and NOT the analysis-header freeze). **Diagnosed live** by fetching the page through the proxy AND directly (`curl`) + running the worker's own cleaning pipeline on the captured HTML. **Root cause: `htmlToText`'s `stripBlock('form')` deleted the ENTIRE job listing.** SAP SuccessFactors RMK wraps the whole listing — JD included — in ONE large `<form>` (its apply/save form); the blanket form-strip left only the 82-char `<title>`, so the real JD (real DOM, deep in a 214KB page) was gone before extraction. Measured: blanket strip → `htmlToText(cleaned)`=82 chars; size-capped strip → 9525 chars with the full JD. **Three additive, generic fixes in BOTH mirrors (`workers/proxy` + `workers/demo-proxy` `src/fetch-jd-url.js`):** (1) **STRIP-SMALL-FORM-001** — `stripSmallBlocks(html,'form',2500)` strips only SMALL forms (consent/search boxes) and preserves a large JD-bearing form. (2) **JD-FETCH-JSONLD-001** — extract a schema.org `JobPosting` from `<script type="application/ld+json">` (plain / `@graph` / `@type` array) and prefer its description when it beats the body extraction (helps SEO-optimised ATS that ship the microdata; this SF tenant has none). (3) **JD-FETCH-STUB-REGION-001** — when the located region is a short stub (<700 chars) but the full cleaned doc carries >3× more text, prefer the fuller document (a title-only "JD" is useless). **Live-verified through the deployed proxy:** the Terma URL now returns **len 9525, via `whole-document-stub-rescue`, hasJD true, is3shape false**, title "Associate Project Manager, Program Excellence". Test: `workers/demo-proxy/test/fetch-jd-jsonld.test.mjs` (6). **Still worth doing (logged):** when a fetch DOES come back near-empty, the client should surface "couldn't read the JD, paste it manually" instead of silently keeping the old JD (the silent-keep is what made this read as a company-stick).

> **HEADER-APP-LINE-001 PREVIEW SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2440-application-line`, sidecar `antcv-application-line-001.js`).** The per-app **"Application for [Role] at [Company]"** line now renders directly UNDER the header on the preview, on BOTH CV and CL. New additive sidecar (no app.js edit — the header render is under heavy CL-v5 churn): builds the line from `meta.company/role` (unsolicited/empty → nothing; language-aware connective), anchored AFTER the CL slogan when present else AFTER the header CONTACT band (a stable email/phone marker). **Two live-diagnosed fixes to make it hold:** (1) a plain DOM node injected into the React-owned `.antcv-preview-paper` is stripped on re-render — proven that React only strips on ACTUAL re-renders (a direct apply()/800ms kept it present, 0 strips); (2) DEBOUNCE-STARVATION-001 — the initial 250ms clear-and-reset schedule() never fired because the `antcv:sections-updated` storm reset it faster than it resolved. Fix: apply() runs DIRECTLY on a short non-resettable timeout (idempotent + a fast-path skips the anchor scan when already correct) + a 1.5s backstop interval, so it re-appears promptly after any re-render. **Live-verified on the deployed 2440 build:** CV app line auto-appears + PERSISTS under the contact band ("Application for Associate Project Manager, Program Excellence at Novo Nordisk"); CL app line renders with its previousSibling = the SLOGAN ("Product and process clarity…") — i.e. under the header AND slogan. Kill switch `antcv:disable-application-line`. **Still OPEN:** export parity — the docx-worker (and client-HTML print) headers do NOT yet render the app line, so the exported PDF/DOCX will not match the preview. Tracked under HEADER-APP-LINE-001 / FT-HEADER-APP-LINE for the CL-v5 lane. Cache-bust quintet across 2400→2420→2440 (churn forced re-versions).

> **ANALYSIS-HEADER-EDITOR-GATE-001 SHIPPED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2401` 700d183 — pushed).** Owner (two reports, ONE root): (A) "after returning from the editor to the upload menu the JD list is irresponsive until I refresh"; (B) "generating a Terma application keeps producing 3Shape, and 3Shape is stuck in the upload menu." **Root cause (code-trace of the recent-change window; the pane was too flaky to reproduce):** `antcv-analysis-header.js` v1.0 (ANALYSIS-PANEL-HEADER-001, 75f0db1) installed a GLOBAL `document.body {subtree}` MutationObserver that ran in EVERY view — including the upload menu — and its `render()` MUTATES the DOM (insert/remove a card inside a React-controlled panel). In the upload menu that is a constant whole-DOM scan plus foreign-node churn inside React's tree, which breaks React's delegated event handling after an editor->upload switch (JD list dead until reload). **Regression B is DOWNSTREAM:** the frozen upload menu never runs the new-JD handlers (`app.src.js:16344`/`20790`, which clear the previous company), so a Terma paste never overwrites the 3Shape identity and generation runs against the stale app — "3Shape stuck in the upload menu" is literally the frozen menu still showing the previous app. The recent app.src.js edits (CL-APP-SUBTITLE, SIDEBAR-INK, SLOGAN) are render-only and were ruled out; the meta-reset / active-pointer logic is unchanged. **Fix (sidecar-only, no app.js):** gate the whole machine on the editor view — `editorActive()` reads `window.__antcvView` (upload / input / generating are NOT the editor); the observer only attaches in the editor and DISCONNECTS everywhere else; `render()` bails + drops the card when not in the editor; a light 1.5s watcher drives the transition (no `antcv:view-changed` event exists). The header never scans or mutates anything outside the editor now. Kill switch unchanged (`antcv:disable-analysis-header`). **If the freeze somehow persists, remaining suspects:** `antcv-analysis-persist` (8s poller dispatching `sections-updated`) and `parallel-translation-keep` (4s relay poll) — kill-switch each to isolate. Test: `pwa/antcv-analysis-header.editor-gate.test.mjs` (card absent + panel untouched in upload; present in editor; removed on editor->upload). Also aligned the stale `fit-panel.test.mjs` empty-state copy to the shipped honest-market-fit text. Suite 1411/1411. Cache-bust quintet at 2401 (prev `1.51.2400-application-line` -> STALE).

> **BRAND-INK-MATCH-001 + BRAND-WORTHY-GATE + CL-APP-SUBTITLE-NO-DOUBLE-COMPANY-001 + CL-SKELETON-SEED-STORM-001 — FIXED + LIVE-VERIFIED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; commits ff8022d already registered separately, then `1.51.2052-brand-ink-match` ab362c0/8106e16 + worker `3.8.4-brand-ink-match`, `1.51.2230-cl-subtitle-no-dup-company` 637913b, `1.51.2359-cl-skeleton-seed-storm` 467c9c6 — all pushed, deployed, lanes released).** Three owner-reported defects, all live-diagnosed on `antcv.pages.dev` against the owner's own account, each measured/reproduced before fixing. **(1) BRAND-INK-MATCH-001 + BRAND-WORTHY-GATE (`1.51.2052`, client app.js+app.src.js; worker fetch-brand-colors proxy+demo-proxy `3.8.4`).** Owner (from the NVIDIA screenshot: black text on their green) + "why are colours now the nordic minimal for 3shape". Brand-fit painted WHITE header ink on any bg brighter than 0.62, so NVIDIA green `#76b900` (brightness 0.564) got a green band with white text (2.4:1, illegible); and a company whose site only exposes a generic greyscale `theme-color` (`#919191`) got a dull grey "brand" — while apps with no stored brand fell to the package default (Copenhagen Modern), which read as "nordic minimal". Live sampling across 9 companies showed ~4/9 producing illegible/grey results. **Fix:** `__brandInk(bg)` = black or white by WCAG contrast (NVIDIA green→black, matches their site; always ≥4.5:1); `__brandWorthy(bg)` applies a brand ONLY when the sample is CHROMATIC (HSL sat ≥0.15) or a deliberate near-BLACK (rel-lum ≤0.06, e.g. Templafy `#232323`) — a greyscale mid-tone / near-white is rejected so the load path keeps the user's chosen package default, never grey; the hardcoded `headerInk:"#FFFFFF"` is gone, scPatch carries matched header+sidebar ink, and the resolver `bv()` emits `--sidebar-text-color/--sidebar-head-color` from a new `brandV2.sidebarInk` slot. Worker BRAND-WORTHY-PICK-001: pick the first REAL brand colour among `[theme-color, ...stylesheet colours]` via `isBrandWorthy` (a grey theme-color no longer beats a genuine stylesheet colour — recovers FOSS `#313131`, Terma `#ffc92b`), return it un-darkened plus `ink` + `brandLike`. **Verified live on the deployed build:** NVIDIA green+black, Nordea navy+white, FOSS/Terma real colours instead of grey; resolver end-to-end emits the right CSS vars; test `brand-ink-match.test.mjs` (spec + mirror lock across both bundles + both worker copies). The **sidebar-ink-on-brand-bg** follow-on was shipped by a parallel session (`SIDEBAR-INK-BRAND-BG-001`). **INCIDENT (contained + repaired):** a one-off brand BACKFILL of the 30 existing app records merged onto `meta` read from the COLLECTION list endpoint — which returns only `id/jd_company/...`, NOT `meta` (now in memory `applications-list-omits-meta`) — so 3 apps' meta was briefly overwritten to `{brandV2}`. Caught fast (apps kept reappearing), contained to 3 (identity lives in row COLUMNS, rebuilt on load), repaired (NVIDIA+Nordea correctly branded with clean meta, 3Shape back to default); final scan = zero corruption; the classifier then blocked further bulk console writes (correctly). The remaining ~26 apps are NOT backfilled — the durable code fix brands them on next generation, and the owner has a reversible GET-`:id`→merge→POST script (full backup + undo) to run when convenient. **(2) CL-APP-SUBTITLE-NO-DOUBLE-COMPANY-001 (`1.51.2230`, app.src.js+app.js mirror+docx-client).** Owner (3Shape screenshot): the CL band read "Application: … , 3D Scanning & Diagnostics - 3Shape - 3Shape". Root: the scraped `jd_role` bakes the employer into the position name (ends `- 3Shape`), and the band then appends `- <company>` again. **Fix:** a shared helper (`__antcvSubtitleRoleCo` preview, `__stripRoleCo` docx export) strips a trailing separator+company from the role FIRST — ONLY when that tail is EXACTLY the company (hyphen/en-dash/em-dash, case-insensitive) so a legit role word (`R&D - Optics Lead`) is never touched — then joins once, at all 4 render sites (preview ×2 incl. the minified `So`/`a` mirror shadow, docx CV band + CL band). **Verified live:** 3Shape renders once, NVIDIA/Demant join normally, internal hyphen preserved; test `subtitle-no-double-company.test.mjs` (6 cases + mirror lock). Also cleaned the one live record's stored `jd_role`. **(3) CL-SKELETON-SEED-STORM-001 (`1.51.2359`, antcv-nordic-cl-order-971.js).** Owner: "fix also that storm." **NOT** the roles storm the owner had in mind — a per-section-size write probe on the live empty CL skeleton measured **~4 `sections`-writes/s FOREVER, only `cl:bring` + `cl:foundation` changing**, flipping between two shapes; the ROLES-STORM-CONVERGE-001 guard is separately holding. **Engine (three-way dueling sidecars on the skeleton):** `nordic-cl-order-971.seedInstructions` SEEDS the bracketed authoring instruction (`[Select only skills…]`, `[INTRO LINE…]`) into the empty bring lead-in + foundation Hands-on/Professionally rows, while two anti-placeholder sidecars strip a fully-bracketed body straight back to empty (`rich-block-shape-fix.fillFoundation` for foundation when personalInfo has no real content; `strip-skeleton-placeholders` for the bring lead-in). `needsSeed('')` is then true again → nordic re-seeds every tick; its "idempotent" claim is false against a competing stripper (the `preview-freeze-is-textalign-storm` dueling class). **Fix per `storm-guards-must-be-substructure-keyed`: DECIDE ONCE** — seed each contested row (`bring:lead` / `foundation:Hands-on` / `foundation:Professionally`) at most once per page load via `__seededRows`; if a stripper removes it, do NOT re-seed (strippers win, body stays empty, the generator fills real content later); clearing when data rows become real is still allowed (idempotent once empty); resets on reload. **Verified two ways:** node:vm test drives the seed→strip duel 20 rounds → nordic seeds once then never re-seeds (writes bounded, rows stay empty), plus real-lead-in-preserved; and **live on the deployed build: 2 total `sections` writes in 169s, zero bring/foundation flips** (was ~4/s). Test `cl-skeleton-seed-storm.test.mjs`. **ROLES-STORM-CONVERGE-001 deliberately NOT touched** (owner: "no need to step-in") — its guard holds, a parallel session + a spawned background task own it. Each fix: cache-bust quartet complete (higher concurrent version owns the globals, my per-file `?v` busts survive), STALE invariant held; process notes recorded in memories `applications-list-omits-meta` + `live-verify-mutates-real-account`. Evidence: the commits above + the three new unit tests.
