> **HEADER-APP-LINE-001 — OPEN, NOT SHIPPED (owner report + live diagnosis, desktop 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; deliberately NOT touched, to coordinate with the active CL-v5 header lane).** Owner: "switching between applications keeps the old application slogan, specialization is Processes • Products • People, and the app line is still not under the header and slogan." **Diagnosis (live, owner’s account):** the "sticky" line is `meta.subtitle`, which is only a copy of the GLOBAL `personalInfo.specialization` ("Processes • Products • People") — identical on every application, so it does not change on switch. The actual CL slogan reset (`SLOGAN-LOAD-SYMMETRIC-001`, in BOTH the topbar + settings switch handlers, `app.src.js` ~47858 / ~41410) IS already in the deployed `app.js` and clears/sets `antcv:clSlogan` per-app — so the tagline mechanism is not the defect; there is no separate slogan-stick bug. **Desired (owner-confirmed via clarifying Q):** a per-app **"Application for [Role] at [Company]"** line rendered directly UNDER the name + slogan, on BOTH the CV and the CL (switch action = topbar Application History). **Why OPEN, not shipped this session:** this is the known multi-surface CL/CV application-subtitle item (see memory `v5-writing-implementation-status`: "do WITH a visual pass"; extends OPEN_REGISTER **row 66 sub-item (n)** "header shows the SPECIALISATION line instead of the APPLICATION line"). It spans the CV + CL header render (`app.src.js`, preview) AND the docx-worker export (parity), needs live VISUAL verification (this session’s browser pane cannot composite frames — screenshots fail), and the header is under ACTIVE concurrent rework by the CL-v5 slogan-headline lane (`1.51.2194-slogan-headline` + successors, `app.js?v=…-cl-v5-structure`). Folding it into that lane in a render-capable session avoids a preview/export-parity break and a cross-session collision. Registered OPEN in OPEN_REGISTER + FEATURES_REGISTRY (FT-HEADER-APP-LINE).

> **LANG-GUARD-KEY-001 FIXED + LIVE-DIAGNOSED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2200-lang-guard-key`, commit eb36a8e).** Owner: "every generation starts in Chinese." Diagnosed live on `antcv.pages.dev` against the owner’s own account — `language="en"`, `sections` were clean English, yet three LOCAL loss-guard caches held Chinese from an earlier zh generation: `antcv:clProseGuard`, `antcv:outcomesGuard`, `antcv:coreCompGuard`, each keyed by application (`company|role`) with NO language dimension. **Mechanism:** the loss-guards re-inject the last real generated content into a later generation’s SKELETON window (empty section → `reapply()`) to prevent loss. Being language-blind, a Chinese cache was restored into a fresh ENGLISH generation during that window, then the real English arrived — "starts in Chinese, switches to English" (same family as APP-SWITCH-CONTENT-LANG-DETECT-001 `1.51.2012`, which fixed the app-switch selector but NOT this generation-time re-injection). **Fix:** append the current output language to each guard’s cache key (`company|role|lang`) via a shared `curLang()` + `appKey()` change in all three guards, so a generation only ever restores from a SAME-LANGUAGE snapshot; a stale zh cache (keyed without a lang suffix) can no longer match an en lookup — the poison is sidestepped and per-language loss protection is preserved. **Verify:** live — for the current app the CJK caches sit under lang-less keys and the new `…|en` lookup does not match them (`newKeyExists:false`) → no restore → real English flows in. New source-lock `pwa/test/unit/lang-guard-key.test.mjs` (12 checks); the guards’ existing behavioural tests updated to the language-keyed bucket contract; full PWA suite 1401/1401. Cache-bust quintet → `1.51.2200-lang-guard-key` (3 guard sidecars ?v bumped; prev `1.51.2194-slogan-headline` → STALE). Additive sidecar-only (no app.js, no worker). Note: the orphaned lang-less zh cache entries are never read again and evict via the 6-bucket cap — no scrub needed.

> **DELETE-CONFIRM-INAPP-001 SHIPPED + LIVE-SERVED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2146` dd4730c — pushed, lane 1.51.2146-1.51.2165 released).** Owner: "i try to press delete and there is no response." **Diagnosed live on the owner account:** the Application-History delete gate (both surfaces — topbar dropdown `app.src.js:41470` + Settings history `47966`, sharing one `[Hl,Ul]` useState) is a NATIVE `confirm()` ("Delete this application? This is permanent."). In an embedded / installed-PWA browser view the native dialog is auto-cancelled — measured `window.confirm()` returns `false` in ~30ms without ever showing — so `if(confirm(...))` never proceeds and the click silently no-ops. Verified the controls were otherwise healthy: 31 delete buttons, none `disabled`, no stuck busy flag, token not expired (so a real-browser delete would have worked). **Fix:** replace the native-confirm gate at BOTH sites with a **two-click in-app confirm** needing no native dialog and no new state — first 🗑 click ARMS the row (`Hl='armdel:'+id`, label -> "Delete?"), a second click within 4s runs the existing delete (`oo.remove` + list refresh), and a `setTimeout` functional-setState auto-disarms so it can never stick; the `disabled` gate is relaxed to `!!Hl && Hl.indexOf('armdel:')!==0` so the armed button stays clickable while real switch/delete ops still lock the row. Mirrored surgically into minified `app.js` at all four spots (both onClick handlers + both labels + the topbar disabled); head unchanged (`(()=>{window`, no `"use strict"`), parses, app.js<->app.src.js mirror-lock + APPJS-BLUESCREEN-001 guards green. Suite **1389/1389**. Cache-bust quintet at 2146 (app.js `?v` + index seed + version-override `?v` + `sw.js` CACHE + TARGET_VERSION; prev `1.51.2126-copenhagen-v2-insets` -> STALE). Live-verified: the served `app.js?v=1.51.2146` carries `armdel:` x11, the two "Delete?" labels, and zero native delete-`confirm()`; it is the build the owner tab loads (a later parallel `1.51.2174` bumped only the display version, not app.js). **Owner UX note:** deleting a saved application is now two deliberate clicks (🗑 -> "Delete?" -> click again) and works in the in-app / PWA browser where the native dialog was being swallowed.

> **PARALLEL-TRANSLATION-KEEP-001 SHIPPED + LIVE-SERVED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2106` 20d45f9 — pushed, lane 1.51.2106-1.51.2125 released).** Owner: "applications generated in multiple languages must not be overwritten — keep a parallel translation." Root of how English CVs became Chinese on disk (same family as APP-SWITCH-CONTENT-LANG-DETECT-001): an app generated in one language and then VIEWED in another (the babel-fish display translate) had its translated view auto-saved over the canonical `cv_sections` in cloud. **Flow-mapped first (subagent):** the ONLY server path that writes `cv_sections` is `PUT /api/applications/:id` (access-relay `index.js:3585` — a per-field whitelist merge, so an OMITTED key preserves the stored canonical; `null`/`[]` would clobber). Per-language translations are ALREADY kept in parallel in the `langRenders` bundle (localStorage + cloud prefs). So the sole remaining defect is the canonical being overwritten by the translated view through the `[ro,io,yo]` auto-sync PUT (`app.src.js:18747`, via the `antcv:sections-updated` listener at 17910). **Fix — a self-contained sidecar `antcv-parallel-translation-keep.js`, modelled on `antcv-cloud-put-shrink-guard-289.js` (a proven request-only fetch guard: inspect/edit the outgoing body, delegate to the original fetch, NEVER touch the Response). ZERO app.js/app.src.js edits — no minified-mirror risk.** Intercepts `PUT /api/applications/:id`; if the body's `cv_sections`/`cl_sections` are in a non-Latin script DIFFERENT from that app's PRIMARY (generation) language, drops those two keys so the relay preserves the canonical; the translation stays in `langRenders`. **Primary learned fail-OPEN:** a read-only GET of the cloud row when the active app changes (its stored canonical's script) + the fresh content's script on a generation completing. Unknown primary NEVER strips — worst case is no protection, never a blocked save or data loss (OMIT, never `null`/`[]` — respects the DATA-LOSS-on-restore family). Only NON-LATIN flips guarded (exactly what `langRenders` parallelises); Latin<->Latin + same-script edits always save. Detection reuses the one vetted `window.__antcvContentScript`, so guard + healer + selector agree by construction. Kill switch `antcv:disable-parallel-translation-keep`. **Test:** `pwa/antcv-parallel-translation-keep.test.mjs` — the full decision table + helpers. Suite **1389/1389**. Cache-bust quintet complete at 2106 (new sidecar `?v` + index.html seed + version-override `?v` + `sw.js` CACHE + TARGET_VERSION; prev `1.51.2086-copenhagen-v2-box` → STALE). Live-verified: wired after babel-relang, guard present, request-only, seed == TARGET.

> **EXPORT-PREVIEW-ZOOM-DECOUPLE-001 FIXED + LIVE-VERIFIED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2085-export-preview-zoom-decouple`, commit 745b88d).** Owner: the export preview (the 📄 preview modal) cut pages by ~half AND its zoom followed the editor preview’s zoom (changing editor zoom rescaled the export preview too). **One root cause, two symptoms:** the live editor bakes its zoom onto the paper as an INLINE `transform: scale(<editor-zoom>); transform-origin: top left` (`app.src.js` ~51102); `antcv-pdf-preview-gate.js` clones each `.antcv-preview-paper` via `p.outerHTML`, so the clone inherited that transform — (1) the editor zoom value rode into the export iframe verbatim (live-confirmed: editor `scale(0.955)` → iframe paper computed `scale(0.955)`), and (2) it stacked on the modal’s own fit zoom (double scale); a transform’s layout box stays full-size while its content shrinks top-left, desyncing the `.antcv-page-row` math so pages chopped ~half. **Fix (gate srcdoc `<style>`):** `.antcv-preview-paper { transform: none !important; transform-origin: top left !important; }` — `!important` stylesheet beats the inline transform, so the export preview starts at natural 100% and does its OWN independent fit via the existing body `zoom: var(--antcv-fit)` (single scale). Covers the initial build AND the doc-toggle rebuild (same iframe stylesheet). **Verify:** source-lock `pwa/test/unit/export-preview-zoom-decouple.test.mjs` (3 checks); full PWA suite 1378/1378. Live on the DEPLOYED `1.51.2085` build: with editor `scale(0.955)`, the iframe clone’s inline transform is still `scale(0.955)` but its COMPUTED transform is `none` (DECOUPLED=true) and the paper is scaled only by the fit `zoom=0.512` — no double scale. Additive CSS-only change (no app.js, no worker).

> **HTML-TO-PDF-001 SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2072-analysis-cloudconvert` commit 60ba37b, docx-worker `1.14.162-analysis-html-pdf` deployed run 29899051626).** Owner: "add the analysis to export with CloudConvert." The JD-analysis report export (`antcv-analysis-report-pdf-360.js`) printed the report through an offscreen-iframe `window.print()` — Chrome’s print pipeline gives an ATS-illegible PDF (no ToUnicode CMap), the same reason the CV/CL export uses the CloudConvert docx-worker. Now the report renders server-side through the SAME worker via CloudConvert’s Chrome HTML→PDF engine. **Worker (`1.14.162`):** new `convertHtmlToPdf()` (import/base64 UTF-8 → convert input=html engine=chrome print_background A4 zero-margin → export/url), inlined in the bundled `src/index.js` + mirrored in `src/cloudconvert.js`; new route `POST /generate-analysis-pdf` → `handleGenerateAnalysisPdf` with the SAME gates as `/generate-pdf` (WORKER_SECRET, allowed-origin CORS, `pdfProvider` 503, BYOK `X-CloudConvert-Key` precedence, 4MB cap), body `{ html, filename }`; CORS allow-headers now include `X-CloudConvert-Key` (fixes a latent BYOK preflight gap `/generate-pdf` shared). **PWA (`1.51.2072`):** `exportPdf()` tries the worker first (`exportViaWorker`, gated by `__antcvUseServerPdf` + `isPdfWorkerAvailable` + `ANTCV_DOCX_WORKER`, BYOK header) and downloads the PDF; the offscreen-iframe browser print is preserved as `printViaIframe()` and used ONLY when the server path is unavailable/fails — so an old worker degrades gracefully. **Verify:** worker unit test `workers/docx-worker/test/html-to-pdf.test.mjs` (mocked-fetch job-shape + route source-lock), worker CI 17/17, full PWA suite 1365/1365. Live: worker `/health` = `1.14.162-analysis-html-pdf` (`pdf_via: cloudconvert`); a direct `POST /generate-analysis-pdf` returned HTTP 200 `application/pdf` 30 KB `%PDF-1.4` (æøå / 分析 unicode OK); and driven end-to-end on the deployed PWA against the owner’s signed-in account — `AntcvAnalysisReportExport()` POSTed to `.../generate-analysis-pdf`, downloaded `JD Analysis - Senior Project Manager - R&D Medical Devices….pdf`, and called `window.print` **0 times**. Cache-bust quintet → `1.51.2072-analysis-cloudconvert` (prev `1.51.2032-micro-compress-ladder` → STALE). Registered FEATURES_REGISTRY FT-ANALYSIS-CLOUDCONVERT + OPEN_REGISTER CLOSED.

> **APP-SWITCH-CONTENT-LANG-DETECT-001 SHIPPED + LIVE-SERVED (desktop session 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.2012` 25a1d65 — pushed, lane 1.51.2012-1.51.2031 released).** Owner: "every CV starts in Chinese and switches to English — zh is the only Chinese CV we should have here." **Regression from `APP-SWITCH-CONTENT-LANG-001` (`1.51.1794`, 1ca51c9)**, which was itself added to fix the OPPOSITE complaint (a Chinese-content app whose selector read English). That fix set the language selector from the content's script with `if (/[CJK]/.test(JSON.stringify(cv_sections+cl_sections).slice(0,4000))) return 'zh'` — **a SINGLE CJK codepoint anywhere returned zh.** Because so many apps carry babel RESIDUE (one stray Chinese character — the exact class the BABEL-RICHBLOCK / TOOLS-residue work has been chasing), every such app flipped to Chinese on load. That also fed a **contamination loop:** residue → selector zh → babel-relang translates the whole doc to zh → persists → cloud-syncs, so more apps became "Chinese" over time. Two faults: (a) one character is not a language; (b) `JSON.stringify` includes the Latin object KEYS (`title`/`company`/`bullets`…), so you cannot rescue it with a ratio over the stringified blob — the keys swamp it. **Fix — detect from the SAME vetted signal babel-relang HEALS with, so the selector and the healer agree by construction** (their disagreement was the original "stuck" bug): new `window.__antcvContentScript(cvSections, clSections)` in `antcv-babel-relang.js` reuses `textOf` (extracts string VALUES, never keys) + `isInLanguage` (prose-ratio ≥ THRESHOLD_PROSE with acronyms / proper nouns / product names excluded, ≥200-letter floor). Returns the wide script the content is genuinely in, or `''` for Latin / residue / too-short (caller then falls back to `jd_language`). Both `app.src.js` sites (settings loader line ~41387 + topbar twin ~47822) call it, with a conservative inline fallback (majority wide-script over lowercase-Latin prose, residue-proof) for the pre-boot window before the sidecar loads. **Mirrored surgically into minified `app.js` at both sites** — head still `(()=>{window` (no `"use strict"`), parses, ALL mirror-lock + APPJS-BLUESCREEN-001 guards green. **Effect:** a genuinely Chinese CV (prose-ratio ~0.4+, measured 0.73 on the test fixture) still selects zh; an English CV with residue stays on its `jd_language` and babel-relang heals the residue to English; the contamination loop is cut at the source, so already-partly-contaminated apps self-heal on next load instead of sticking Chinese. **Test:** `pwa/antcv-babel-relang.contentscript.test.mjs` (genuine zh → zh; en+residue → ''; clean en → ''; too-short → ''; CL-half residue → ''). Suite **1370/1370**. Cache-bust quintet complete at 2012 (app.js + babel-relang `?v` + version-override `?v` + ANTCV_VERSION seed + `sw.js` CACHE + TARGET_VERSION; prev `1.51.1992-slogan-cap-4-13` → STALE, invariant held). Live-verified on antcv.pages.dev: app.js + babel-relang both served at 2012, the single-char detector is GONE (0 occurrences), both app.js sites call the helper, the sidecar exports it, seed == TARGET (no version regression / no stale-SW mask). **Note:** this and `APP-SWITCH-CONTENT-LANG-001` are duals — the correct invariant is that the app-switch selector and babel-relang must use ONE detector; they now do, so neither the "selector says English over Chinese content" nor the "every CV starts Chinese" failure can recur from a detector mismatch.

> **WORKER-HEALTH-ATTEST-HOSTNAME-001 — the recurring "worker `/health` DNS-gated / owed" note was a WRONG-HOSTNAME error, not a network gate; all four workers now attested live (desktop nightly 2026-07-22 #2, Opus 4.8; no code ship).** For ~5 sweeps (07-13→07-22) the nightly reports claimed the desktop/CI shell "cannot resolve `*.workers.dev`" and left the worker `/health` trio "owed to an un-gated run". This run proves the resolve failure was a **wrong `workers.dev` subdomain**: `antcv-access-relay.gabriel-a-karp.workers.dev/health` → HTTP **000** (could not resolve host), while the CORRECT `antcv-access-relay.`**`karp-gabriel-a`**`.workers.dev/health` → **200**. The correct subdomain is confirmed against `pwa/*` URLs and the relay's own `/health` `endpoints` self-report. With the right host the desktop shell reaches every worker; **all four attested live and all match in-repo source VERSION → no drift:** access-relay `auth-34-category-downgrade` (clears the CATEGORY-DOWNGRADE-VERSION-BUMP-001 eyeball owed since 07-19), docx-worker `1.14.161-leadin-underline` (clears the LEAD-UNDERLINE-VERSION-BUMP-001 eyeball owed since 07-16), cv-proxy + demo-proxy `3.8.3-gemini-flash-ramble`. **Action for future sweeps:** attest via `*.karp-gabriel-a.workers.dev`; stop reporting `/health` as gated. Standing probes green on the same base (suite 1365/1365; boot-smoke/button-audit/panel-probes cited from the CI dispatch on this identical commit). No production code touched. Evidence: `docs/qa/NIGHTLY_2026-07-22_REPORT.md` (DESKTOP DISPATCH #2) + OPEN_REGISTER 2026-07-22 DESKTOP DISPATCH block.

> **DIAG-PROBE-WINPATH-001 LANDED ON MAIN + DIAG-PROBE-NO-META-001 (new) — row-17 lock probes made CI-runnable (GitHub Actions nightly 2026-07-22, Opus 4.8; test-infra only, pushed DIRECT-TO-MAIN on `1.51.1972` base).** The two standing settings-panel stability probes — `pwa/test/diag-personal-panel-probe.mjs` + `pwa/test/diag-settings-panels-probe.mjs` (register row 17) — were **un-runnable in CI** for three compounding reasons, all fixed this run; no `app.js`/`app.src.js`/`worker` touched. **(1) DIAG-PROBE-WINPATH-001 (now landed):** both hardcoded `const ROOT = 'C:/Users/karpg/GitHub/AntCV/pwa'` → on a Linux clone the in-process static server 404s every asset → the app never boots → blank page. This is the *identical* bug the 07-21 CI dispatch fixed on branch `nightly-2026-07-21-probe-portable-path` (commit 2a2b9850), but that PR was org-policy-blocked ("GitHub Actions is not permitted to create or approve pull requests"), was **never merged**, and so never reached main — the probes stayed broken. Fixed here with the portable `const ROOT = path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..')` pattern already used by `diag-panel-button-audit.mjs`. **(2) DIAG-PROBE-NO-META-001 (new):** even with a correct ROOT the editor now renders a **blank body** (0 buttons, no `⚙` gear) unless `localStorage.meta` is seeded — diagnosed directly (with `meta:{company,role}` → 470 els / 24 buttons / gear present; without → empty `body.innerText`). `diag-panel-button-audit.mjs` seeds `meta` (why it worked); the two panel probes did not. Both now seed it. **(3) `⚙` gear click unreachable (new):** the gear `<button>⚙</button>` carries an emoji variation-selector that defeats `text=⚙` / `getByText('⚙')` / `hasText:'⚙'`, and a real-mouse `.click()` times out on the continuously re-rendering toolbar's actionability/stability wait — instrumented proof: the button is visible + uncovered (`elementFromPoint===self`) and a **DOM `el.click()` opens the panel** (WRITING STYLE / Personal / Account / Layout appear). Both the gear open and the subtab opens are now driven by a node click via `page.evaluate`. **Hardening:** the personal probe fails **cleanly** (`DIAG FAIL — Personal panel never opened`) instead of crashing at `Object.entries(undefined)`; the settings probe's PASS now **requires** the modal actually opened (`openedSettings` + per-subtab `rootFound`) so a blank page can no longer vacuously PASS. **Verified (Linux CI):** `node --check` clean on both; personal **DIAG PASS** 0 mut/8s and settings **DIAG PASS** Personal+Account+Layout each 0 mut/6s **rootFound=true**, both across **3 consecutive runs** (non-flaky), 0 page errors; suite **1365/1365** unaffected (these diag probes are standalone). **PR-vs-direct:** the CI override forces a PR only for `app.js`/`app.src.js`/`workers/**`; these are pure CI-harness files (not loaded by the PWA, no `?v`, zero production/deploy risk) and the PR route already failed to land the identical fix — pushed direct-to-main to restore row-17 coverage. **Owner:** delete the now-superseded branch `nightly-2026-07-21-probe-portable-path`. Evidence: `docs/qa/NIGHTLY_2026-07-22_REPORT.md` + OPEN_REGISTER 2026-07-22 CI-DISPATCH block.

> **BABEL-RICHBLOCK-RESIDUE-CONVERGE-001 SHIPPED + LIVE-SERVED (desktop session 2026-07-21, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.1972` 8b2a52a — pushed, lane 1.51.1972-1.51.1991 released).** Closes the follow-up left open by ROLES-STORM-CONVERGE-001 (`1.51.1902`): **the rich_block Latin lead-in residue drop is RE-ENABLED.** History — the drop shipped 2026-07-11 and was DISABLED the same day: on a zh/he/ar ribbon it removed the stored English `Foundation: I connect what I do best…` twin from a rich_block, a LEGACY RE-ADDER (foundation-758 pre-345 caches, shape-guard eager writes, languageCache echoes) put it straight back, and the pair cycled about every 5s — owner: "preview jumpy / edit closes", the editor unmounting mid-edit. The recorded re-entry condition was "only after the re-adder inventory is complete". **That condition is not reachable and was the wrong gate:** the inventory is open-ended, and a NEW re-adder would reopen the war even if it were finished. **Fix — make the drop remover-agnostic instead, reusing the pattern that just converged the roles storm:** (1) **STICKY decision** — drop a given residue set ONCE per (doc × ribbon language × the content that SURVIVES the drop). If the identical residue is back while everything else is byte-identical, a re-adder owns those rows: hold, log once, and let the wide-script twin stand beside the English row rather than flicker the editor. The key is derived from what survives, so the add/drop churn itself cannot move it, while a genuine edit or translate pass does — which re-arms the drop. (2) **The write-side substructure guard is GENERALISED** from experience-only to ANY contested section, across BOTH docs (`cv` + `cl`), keyed by section id/type; one belt losing a tug-of-war no longer blocks the other belts' work in the same pass, and a duplicated section key is treated as unusable and never held. (3) Kill switch `antcv:disable-richblock-residue-drop`. **Layering matters and is measured:** with the sticky guard neutered the harness goes back to **10 of 20 passes writing** (the per-section write guard alone only halves the storm); with both layers it is **≤ 2**. **No conflict with CL-V5-FOUNDATION-KEEP-001 / -HOLD-001 (`1.51.1944`/`1.51.1945`, the parallel session):** those keep the foundation SECTION visible while it holds real prose; this drops a duplicate Latin ROW inside a rich_block, and the pass's `hasWide` gate guarantees the block always keeps at least one item, so it can never empty a section. **Test:** `pwa/antcv-richblock-residue.storm.test.mjs` — same Node shim + FAKE 2.5s clock — covers drop-on-zh, no-drop-on-en (wide-script only), the kill switch, convergence under a re-adder with parallel churn, and re-arming after a genuine translate change. Suite **1365/1365**. Cache-bust quintet complete at 1972 (prev `1.51.1945-cl-foundation-hold` → STALE, invariant held). Live-verified: index.html references `antcv-sections-normalize-415.js?v=1.51.1972-richblock-residue-converge`, the served sidecar contains the sticky guard + the per-section guard + the ENABLED call sites, and the ANTCV_VERSION seed matches TARGET (no version regression / no stale-SW mask). **Behaviour note for the owner:** on a wide-script ribbon the leftover English lead-in row now disappears on its own; if some belt keeps re-adding it, it stays visible after one attempt and says so once in the console — it will never cycle again.

> **EXPORT-PDF-PANEL-WORKER-001 E2E LIVE-DRIVEN — CLOSED (desktop session 2026-07-21, Opus 4.8; no code ship — verification only, on live `antcv.pages.dev` at `1.51.1943-cl-v5-durable`).** The 07-19 nightly register-reconcile logged this fix (`1.51.1556`, bea9cf6) as shipped + suite-covered, but the one leg nobody had driven was the actual end-user click-through. Driven now against the owner’s own signed-in account and the real docx-worker. **Deployment attested:** `antcv-pdf-preview-gate.js?v=1.51.1556-pdf-panel-worker` served AND running (`window.__antcvPdfPreviewGateInstalled === "1.51.1556-pdf-panel-worker"`); the `app.js` policy exposure SURVIVED the later app.js re-touches (live `app.js?v=1.51.1922-cl-v5-structure` still yields `typeof window.__antcvUseServerPdf === "function"` and `__antcvDemoActive === "function"`, evaluating `__antcvUseServerPdf() === true` on the owner’s own-worker deployment). **Root cause reproduced live, exactly as diagnosed:** on the Preview tab `button[title^="Export as PDF"]` is present; after clicking the `🎯 Analysis` tab it is GONE (`false`, and so is the DOCX button) while `.antcv-preview-paper` still renders (1) and the export FAB stays visible (`display:flex`) — i.e. the user can still reach the export modal from a tab where the app’s own export buttons are unmounted. **Drive result (spies on both branches; `window.print` neutralised so no blocking dialog could hang the pane):** clicking the modal’s "Save as PDF" with the Analysis panel open produced `Preparing… [disabled]` (the new feedback path), then `exportPdfViaWorker(doc=cv)` → a real **POST `https://docx-worker.karp-gabriel-a.workers.dev/generate-pdf`**, the modal auto-closed on success (the resolve path = PDF delivered), and **`window.print` was called 0 times**. Payload `watermark: ""` — correct, the owner is not a demo user. Pre-fix this exact state went straight to browser print. **Env note (not a defect):** the export modal stalls at "Building export preview…" in the automation pane because its double-`requestAnimationFrame` build never fires in a throttled/occluded tab (the known `island-raf-freeze` class); shimming `rAF`→`setTimeout` let the real build run. Worth considering a `setTimeout` fallback in `antcv-pdf-preview-gate.js` so the modal cannot hang for a real user in a backgrounded tab — **filed as the only follow-up**, no evidence of a live user hitting it. All spies, the `rAF` shim and the tab state were restored afterwards (verified: worker fn restored, `window.print` restored, `rAF` restored, modal removed, back on Preview with the export button present). No account data mutated — export is read-only + a download.

> **SAVED-APPLICATION LIFECYCLE CLUSTER — 4 owner-reported defects FIXED + LIVE-VERIFIED (desktop session 2026-07-20/21, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.1708` 35b6120, `1.51.1800` 1ca51c9, `1.51.1882` 492ca1e + relay 0af16b9; cv-proxy `105bfd9c` / demo-proxy `c37d0871` / access-relay `27def9ec` deployed; lanes released).** Four separate owner reports that all trace to the saved-application (History / JD-list) path. **(1) SWITCH-OPEN-JDONLY-001 (`1.51.1708`, 35b6120) — Settings → Application History → Switch (and the topbar twin) refused to open MANY apps** with "This saved draft has no stored content…", e.g. `#796 Ibsen Photonics`. Root, confirmed by a direct relay fetch: the cloud row is **JD-only** — `GET /api/applications/796` returns 200 with `jd_text`/`meta`/`jd_company`/`style_config` but `cv_sections:null` and `cl_sections:null`. Both handlers gated on `Array.isArray(n.cv_sections)&&length` (app.src.js ~41267 settings + ~47692 topbar) → `__hasReal` false → the dead-end toast (the DATA-LOSS-LOAD-GRACE-001 guard). The cloud-restore filter is NOT involved — `antcv-cloud-restore-filter-298` only touches `/api/prefs`. **Fix:** widen `__hasReal` to also open a record carrying a JD/identity (`jd_text` / `jd_company` / `meta.company|role`). Safe by construction: a FAILED fetch returns no `t.application` (handled by the enclosing `if`), so only a SUCCESSFUL fetch of a genuinely section-less row reaches here; the prior app is already saved at the top of the handler; and the me() minimum-sections floor supplies the template — so the user lands on the app (identity + JD loaded) ready to Generate instead of a dead end. Mirrored into minified app.js (`__hasReal` name survives minification; replace_all, both sites). **(2) WIPE-NONDESTRUCTIVE-RESTORE-001 (relay, 0af16b9) — the ROOT of "why do so many apps have null sections".** `POST /api/prefs/wipe-generated` deliberately NULLs the ACTIVE app's `cv/cl_sections` at the START of a full regen (so the old CV can't seed the fresh generation); if that regen is abandoned / errors / the save mis-routes on company drift, the row is left **permanently null**. NOT the autosave — the backend already blocks empty-array-over-populated writes (`__blockCvBlank`, index.js ~3483) and updates are partial, so a client-side sections-preserve guard was built and then **reverted as redundant**. **Fix (safe-by-construction — only ADDS recovery to an already-null row; contamination behaviour unchanged):** `ALTER TABLE application ADD COLUMN cv_sections_bak / cl_sections_bak` (run live on remote `ant_memory`; documented in `schema.sql`); the wipe SNAPSHOTs live sections into `*_bak` before nulling (best-effort + OUTSIDE the batch, so a pre-migration DB just nulls exactly as before; only snapshots when current sections are LIVE, so re-wiping an already-null row can't clobber a good backup); and the EXPLICIT single-app GET `handleApiApplicationById` — **never** the `/api/prefs` active_application seed, else a mid-regen sync would re-apply the backup and re-seed the very contamination the wipe prevents — restores from `*_bak` when live sections are empty, self-heals the row and returns it. **Verified e2e on live D1:** simulated wipe on #796 (cv→NULL, bak=18011 chars) → app GET → 15 cv + 8 cl sections restored, `bak` cleared. #796 itself was a pre-fix casualty (nulled before the backup existed). **(3) DEDUP-ROLE-NORMALIZE-001 (`1.51.1800` 1ca51c9 + relay) — duplicate applications in History** (Aimpoint ×3, Demant ×4, 3Shape ×2). Two mechanisms: the `UNIQUE(user_hash, jd_hash)` key goes STALE vs `jd_text` (proven — two Demant rows have byte-identical `jd_text`, middle and tail, yet different hashes), and the employer+role dedup (`shouldDedupeByJob` → exact `jd_role = ?`, index.js ~3315) missed role-string VARIANTS of the same job — en-dash vs hyphen and a trailing `- <company>` suffix (3Shape `"Senior Project Manager – R&D…"` vs `"…- R&D… - 3Shape"`, which duplicated the same day). **Fix:** `dedupeRoleKey(role, company)` normalises (unify dash variants, strip a trailing `- <company>` / `at <company>`, punctuation→space) and the create handler fetches candidates by employer then matches the NORMALIZED key, so variants UPDATE the existing row. **Cleanup:** `dedup-cleanup.mjs` (same normaliser) merged **11 duplicate rows** — kept the freshest-WITH-CONTENT per group, never deleted the active app, and deliberately EXCLUDED empty-role groups (the Chinese `主动申请` unsolicited placeholder — an empty role key cannot distinguish two genuinely different applications). Verified **0 dup groups remain**. **(4) APP-SWITCH-CONTENT-LANG-001 (`1.51.1800`, 1ca51c9) — a Chinese-content app rendered Chinese but the top-bar selector stayed English, and later loads "stuck" Chinese.** Both load handlers set the UI language from `jd_language` — which is the **JOB's** language (`"en"` for an English/Danish posting) and NOT the language the CV is written in. Confirmed live: `#796` and `#2708` carry `jd_language:"en"` with profile title `个人简介`, while `#807` is correctly English; a zh translate/generation persisted zh sections without touching `jd_language` (which is semantically correct — it describes the JD). **Fix:** detect the CONTENT's script from `cv/cl_sections` (CJK / Hebrew / Ethiopic) and set the selector from that, falling back to `jd_language` for Latin/empty content, so the bar always matches what the CV is written in. Mirrored into minified app.js (both sites). **This is the ENTRY-point half of the Chinese-stuck family; the CONTENT-heal half is BABEL-EN-ASYMMETRY-001 / BABEL-LATIN-BLIND-001 (`1.51.1818`/`1.51.1838`) from the parallel session — complementary, not conflicting** (this runs only on load, so it cannot oscillate with the relang heal). **Process notes:** the rAF-frozen-pane trap bit again — both the in-app Browser pane and an automation-driven Chrome report `visibilityState:hidden`, so rAF never fires and the whole pagination/render pipeline is dead; synchronous reads, `fetch`, and `AntcvAutoPagebreak._compute()` still work, and diag counters must be persisted to a localStorage key to survive the tab losing focus (see the CAUTION in `preview-freeze-is-textalign-storm`). A same-`?v` **version collision** with a parallel session (both claimed 1.51.1794) forced a re-bump to 1795 then 1800 — when the version files conflict on rebase, take the HIGHER origin version and bump ABOVE it, never downgrade. Evidence: commits 35b6120 / 0af16b9 / 1ca51c9 + live D1 probes.

> **LINKEDIN-CARD-EXTRACT-001 FIXED + OWNER-CONFIRMED (desktop session 2026-07-21, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.1882` 492ca1e; cv-proxy `105bfd9c` + demo-proxy `c37d0871` deployed; lane 1.51.1882-1.51.1901 released).** Owner: "the manual add in the JD list lost its ability to extract company and position for fill-in in the table, at least when it is from LinkedIn." **Root:** the LinkedIn **guest jobPosting FRAGMENT** that `linkedin-guest-rewrite` fetches (`/jobs-guest/jobs/api/jobPosting/<id>`) has **NO `<title>` tag** and no JSON-LD, so `extractTitle()` returned `''` — and the tracker derived Company by splitting the page title, so both prompts pre-filled empty. Verified live against job `4439533198`. **Fix — `extractLinkedInCard(html, bodyText)` in BOTH `workers/{proxy,demo-proxy}/src/fetch-jd-url.js`** (the two copies are independent — patch both; called AFTER `text` is built so the body is available; the response gains a `company` field): role ← `<h2 class="…top-card-layout__title | topcard__title…">`, company ← `<a class="…topcard__org-name-link…">` (fallback `span.topcard__flavor`). **Recruiter caveat (owner-confirmed: "it makes sense the company name is DTU Wind"):** for an agency posting `org-name-link` is the AGENCY ("PMs for Hire") while the REAL employer is appended to the title as `"… - DTU Wind"`. So prefer the title's TRAILING dash-segment as the company when it is ≤5 words **AND appears verbatim in the JD body** — a strong signal it is the hiring employer rather than part of the role — and strip it from the role; a DIRECT posting has no such suffix, so `org-name` (already the real employer) is kept. Client: `src/islands/JobTracker/api.ts` `JdFetch` exposes `company`; `JobTracker.tsx` uses `jd.company` for the Company prompt with the old title-split guess retained as fallback. **LIVE VERIFIED against the deployed cv-proxy:** `title="Project Manager - Operations and development of National test centres"`, `company="DTU Wind"` (agency correctly ignored); owner confirmed "DTU Wind fetched". **Process hazard recorded:** the ISLANDS bundle is VITE-built (`npm run build` → `pwa/antcv-react-islands.js`) and its `?v` in index.html had **drifted stale at `1.51.1794` while the app was at `1.51.1838`** — island-only changes would not have cache-busted. Bumped in step with the quintet; worth checking whenever an island change appears not to land. Evidence: commit 492ca1e + live proxy probe.

> **ROLES-STORM-CONVERGE-001 FIXED + LIVE-SERVED (desktop session 2026-07-21, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.1902` 408cc5c — pushed, lane 1.51.1902-1.51.1921 released).** Owner-measured on `antcv.pages.dev` `1.51.1792` (Ibsen Photonics app, owner account, **at rest**): `sections` written ~40x in 31s (1.27 writes/sec) FOREVER, the console looping `[415] experience-completeness restored 3 missing role(s) hidden` + `[sections-normalize-415] re-applied normalisers ... after restore`. This is the ROLES leg of the same family as TOOLS-GRP-STORM-CONVERGE-001 (`1.51.1772`, ff8022d) — that one is fixed and all three tools belts are silent, so the remaining churn was entirely the roles path. **Mechanism:** `repairExperienceCompleteness` re-added the same 3 PI roles as HIDDEN every pass and a competing writer stripped them straight back out. **Neither existing guard could converge it, for structural reasons:** (a) the add-side `__complRepeat` counter is TIME-windowed (3 hits / 6s) against a **2.5s poll**, so it suppressed at most one pass in three and then re-armed — and it `= null`-RESET on any pass that found nothing missing, which is exactly the state right after its own restore lands, so a strict add/remove alternation held it at n=1 forever; (b) the write-side `__recentWrites` guard hashes the **WHOLE serialised blob**, and a PARALLEL writer (the zh/English translation-residue pass — the app was in Chinese with leftover English tools rows) touched unrelated bytes every cycle, so the hash never repeated and that guard never fired once. **Fix (two legs, both remover-agnostic — they converge WITHOUT identifying the competing writer):** (1) **add-side, STICKY** — restore a given missing-set once per (document × VISIBLE experience substructure); the second time the same set is missing while the visible CV is byte-identical, a competing writer owns those roles, so hold and log once. The churn only toggles HIDDEN roles, so the key is stable across the loop, while a genuine edit/regeneration changes it and re-arms; a page reload re-arms too. Costs the user nothing — the restored roles are hidden either way (recover-in-one-click). (2) **write-side, SUBSTRUCTURE-KEYED** — a second oscillation guard keyed on the EXPERIENCE SECTION alone: re-producing an experience section we wrote seconds ago means it was reverted in between, so keep the STORED one and still write everything else the pass normalised. A parallel translation write can no longer defeat the guard. **Test:** `pwa/antcv-sections-normalize-415.storm.test.mjs` drives the REAL sidecar in a Node shim with a **fake 2.5s clock**, a competing remover and parallel churn — a synchronous test loop hides this entire bug class. **RED pre-fix: 14 of 20 passes wrote** (matches the owner's ~1.27/s); **GREEN post-fix: ≤ 2**, and a regeneration still re-arms the restore. Full pwa suite **1334/1334**. Cache-bust quintet complete at 1902 (index.html normalize-415 `?v` + version-override own `?v` + ANTCV_VERSION seed + `sw.js` CACHE + TARGET_VERSION; prev `1.51.1882-linkedin-card` → STALE, invariant held). Live-verified: the deployed sidecar at that `?v` contains both guards (`ROLES-STORM-CONVERGE-001` × 7, `__complDone`, `__recentExpWrites`). **STILL OPEN / not done this session:** (i) the competing writer was **never identified live** — the Browser pane tab reports `visibilityState: hidden` (timers intensively throttled) and the app loaded there is the NVIDIA one, which is CONVERGED (0 `sections` writes in 20s with a `setItem` probe installed, 0 writes across 12 manually driven `_normalize()` passes); reaching the Ibsen app means loading it from Application history, which mutates the owner's real active-app state (see auto-memory `live-verify-mutates-real-account`), so it was not done. The fix does not depend on that identification, but a future session with a VISIBLE pane should name the writer — candidates ruled OUT by reading: `antcv-role-merge-stored` (stamped one-shot, hides rather than deletes), `antcv-role-merge-dedup` and `antcv-roles-active-floor` (never delete, and both LOG — the owner's console showed no third logger). (ii) **BABEL-RICHBLOCK-RESIDUE-001 remains DISABLED** in 415 (it entered its own write war) and the stored zh/English tools residue is untouched — still the translate pass's job. Auto-memory added: `storm-guards-must-be-substructure-keyed`.

> **TOOLS-GRP-STORM-CONVERGE-001 + BABEL-EN-ASYMMETRY-001 + BABEL-LATIN-BLIND-001 FIXED + LIVE-VERIFIED (desktop session 2026-07-21, Opus 4.8; PWA `1.51.1772` ff8022d, `1.51.1818` efe50be, `1.51.1838` 611d6ea — all pushed, lanes released).** Three owner-reported defects, live-diagnosed on `antcv.pages.dev` against the owner's own account. **(1) TOOLS-GRP-STORM-CONVERGE-001 (`1.51.1772`, ff8022d) — a TOOLS & METHODS group heading flapped in/out at rest.** Unmasked (not caused) by PAGINATION-STABILIZE-001 `1.51.1704`; a fresh empty load showed zero mutations, so it is state-specific. **Mechanism (three-way, engine = a DELETE):** `antcv-tools-corecomp-dedup.js` SPLICED out a TOOLS row whose lead-in label word-matched a CORE COMPETENCIES focus area (live: tools row `Validation` vs focus area `Validation and compliance`, a `sig()` subset match). That delete (a) shifted every following index, so the SECTION-LEVEL rich_block `hidden[]` map pointed at the wrong rows and `__grpHasChild` (app.src.js ~6562, GROUP-EMPTY-HIDE-001) flipped → the heading blinked; and (b) made that kernel category's tokens VANISH from the section, so `antcv-tools-hidden-residue` re-created a `Hidden - <cat>` residue row, whose JD-passing tokens `antcv-sidebar-relevance-cut`'s HEAL pass then RESURRECTED as a visible row — which dedup deleted again. Loop. Console showed all three belts re-firing continuously (`dropped 1 TOOLS row(s)` / `reconciled TOOLS & METHODS` / `JD-relevance cut applied`) with the main thread so saturated that `javascript_tool` timed out. **Fix:** dedup now HIDES the duplicate via the section-level `hidden` map instead of deleting it (sections-hide-over-delete). Indices never shift and the tokens stay in the residue reconciler's view, so no residue twin is created and heal has nothing to resurrect — a true fixpoint. Preview (`app.src.js:6577`) and the docx worker (`src/index.js:27766`) both already drop section-hidden rich rows, so **render + export are byte-identical to the old delete**. Idempotent (already-hidden rows skipped) + an OSC-GUARD holds a re-write of a serialised state written <4s ago (storm-osc-guard family). **Verified live on the deployed belt, English path, via an in-memory fixture:** 5 consecutive `run()` calls → **1 write**, rowCount 5→5 (no index shift), dup row present with `hidden:{"3":true}`, 1 distinct state; and on the owner's real app **0 group-heading flaps / 1 distinct tools-hidden state over 31s**, all three tools belts silent. Tests: `residue-dedup-loop.test.mjs` updated to the hide semantics + a new idempotency case (4/4); `group-empty-hide` 29/29 and `tools-hidden-residue` 16/16 unregressed. **(2)+(3) The CV was stuck in Chinese — owner: "a chinese job was loaded but the language selector stayed english - resulting with chinese all the time".** Live probe confirmed the desync: `language`=`"en"` and `uiLang`=`en` (the selector WAS English), the loaded JD was the ENGLISH NVIDIA posting, yet `sections` carried 3120 CJK chars. **Root cause — `antcv-babel-relang.js` `isInLanguage()` opened with `if (L === 'en') return true;`** ("English is canonical"), so the ONE sidecar that heals a wrong-language document was BLIND in the en direction: a zh rendering under an `en` ribbon passed as already-correct and was NEVER re-rendered — permanently stuck, no reload could recover it (the reverse, English under a zh ribbon, was always caught). **This also DROVE the 415 role storm** the owner reported as separate: with zh section roles and an ENGLISH `personalInfo`, `repairExperienceCompleteness`'s `_samePosition` misses cross-script, so it re-added the same 3 "missing" roles every pass forever (measured ~1.3 `sections` writes/sec at rest, continuous re-render). **Language desync and that storm are ONE bug** — check the ribbon/content match before re-engineering 415's oscillation guard. **BABEL-EN-ASYMMETRY-001 (`1.51.1818`)** made the en test symmetric (English content must not be dominated by a non-Latin script, measured on translatable prose via a shared `proseLatinLen`). **BABEL-LATIN-BLIND-001 (`1.51.1838`)** closed the rest after the owner asked "make sure no similar issue in danish spanish or others" — there was: the en-only fix left FIVE mismatches invisible because the Latin branch only ever measured ENGLISH residue — **da or es content under an `en` ribbon** (a Danish or Spanish job strands the CV exactly like the Chinese one), **da↔es either way** (no English markers to measure), and **zh content under a da/es ribbon** (CJK carries no English markers, so it scored as a clean Danish render). All five reproduced against the shipped detector. Fix: identify the Latin language POSITIVELY — every Latin ribbon (not just `en`) rejects non-Latin-dominated content; near-disjoint function-word sets per en/da/es (deliberately excluding `for`/`en`/`de`/`over`/`under`, which collide across all three) plus orthography (`æøå` Danish, `ñ¿¡áéíóú` Spanish) counted **only inside lowercase-initial prose words**, so capitalised proper nouns (Ørsted, København, José, Muñoz) stay INVARIANTS and an English CV listing Danish employers is not misread as Danish. A foreign language must clear BOTH an absolute floor and a share of the ribbon language's score. **Thresholds are MEASURED, not guessed** (hits/1000 letters, foreign/own ratio): keep — clean da/es, da+English tool invariants, an English quoted publication title, English CV with Danish employers → foreign ≤1.5, ratio ≤0.03; heal — half-translated da+en / es+en → foreign ≥18.6, ratio ≥0.39; **6 / 0.20 sits in that gap** with wide margin both ways, so invariant residue can never fire a costly LLM re-translate while a genuinely mixed render always does. `EN_MARKER_RE`/`EN_RESIDUE_MAX` removed (folded into `LATIN_MARKERS.en`); the half-translated case they used to catch (BABEL-FISH-HEADLESS-001) is covered by the new rule and locked by a test. The `VERSION` bump also fires the built-in one-time `langRenders` purge, dropping the stale zh cache. **Verified — clean A/B on the LIVE deployed build:** `1.51.1818` → **6 failures**, `1.51.1838` → **0/16** (full mismatch matrix + clean-content + half-translated + false-positive battery, run against the real deployed detector in the page). Tests: `babel-en-asymmetry.test.mjs` extended to **14 cases** executing the detector in `node:vm`; `babel-relang-cache`, `lang-fabrication-guard`, `lang-modal-ghost-tap`, `lang-prompt-name`, `languages-prompt-pin` all unregressed (32/32). **COMPLEMENTARY, NOT CONFLICTING — the ENTRY point was fixed independently the same day by a parallel session: APP-SWITCH-CONTENT-LANG-001 (`1.51.1794`, 1ca51c9)** — app load set the selector from `jd_language` (the JOB's language) instead of the CV's content script. Theirs makes the selector tell the truth on load (runs only on load, so no oscillation with this); this one heals CONTENT when the ribbon genuinely is English — the actual recovery path back to English. **Heals only in a VISIBLE editor tab** (RELANG-SINGLE-FLIGHT-001), so a backgrounded pane reporting `document.hidden` blocking the heal is CORRECT, not a failure; the owner's document re-renders to English on their next visible load. **Caveat carried forward:** a background task was spawned for the 415 storm BEFORE this root cause was known and still carries the outdated "separate, pre-existing storm" framing — it should verify the storm is gone after the language heal before re-engineering 415. **Also recorded (process):** live-verifying by monkey-patching a GLOBAL the app reads (`localStorage`) is unsafe on the authenticated pane — AntCV's always-on sidecar swarm read the synthetic fixture mid-window and a probe right after read `cv.length===3` instead of 15; the app's own restore/normalize + kernel floor healed it within seconds and `cloud-put-shrink-guard` + `cloud-restore-filter` (both loaded) are what stop a shrunken blob reaching cloud, but that was luck, not design — prefer a pure entry point or an offline `node:vm` test (which is what actually proved the corecomp fix). Cache-bust quartets complete at each version; STALE_VERSIONS invariant held throughout (each prior TARGET added, current never). Evidence: commits ff8022d / efe50be / 611d6ea + `pwa/test/unit/residue-dedup-loop.test.mjs` + `pwa/test/unit/babel-en-asymmetry.test.mjs`.

> **REGISTER-RECONCILE — SIDEBAR-RICHBLOCK-NOJUSTIFY-001 (antcv-nightly 2026-07-21 2nd dispatch, Opus 4.8 — no code ship; docs-only reconcile of a shipped-but-unregistered day-session app.js render fix).** Since the earlier 07-21 reconcile (baseline `1.51.1644`) the day session shipped through PWA `1.51.1683` — and one leg, **SIDEBAR-RICHBLOCK-NOJUSTIFY-001 (`1.51.1664`, commit b087dfb)**, was in NO register (ACTIVE_BUGS / FEATURES_REGISTRY / OPEN_REGISTER all missed it), a rule-7 gap. It is the render-SOURCE root of the same certs/interests/tools **left↔justify flap** the ALIGN-STORM / STORM-OSCILLATION-GUARD family (07-21, entry below) treated at the symptom layer. **The bug:** `__rowAlign` defaulted a FLAT (non-grouped) `rich_block`'s content rows to `"justify"`; in the narrow sidebar column justify over-stretches into ugly inter-word gaps, and `dejustifyNarrowSidebar` flips it back to `"left"` every pass — a left↔justify flap that reproduces on the template CV **independent of pagination** (owner-reported via screenshots: CERTIFICATES & COURSES, INTERESTS, TOOLS & METHODS). **The fix (at source, `app.src.js:6547`):** sidebar content rows default to `"left"` (`(__hasGrp || S)`, S = the sidebar flag) so there is nothing for the de-justify pass to flip; main-column flat rich_blocks keep justify; grouped rich_blocks unchanged; explicit per-row CJLR still wins. Mirrored surgically into minified `app.js` (`(hg||N)?"left":"justify"`) — **verified this run:** app.js marker present ×1, app.src.js marker present ×1 (both singular), suite's app.js↔app.src.js mirror tests green. Cache-bust quintet complete at 1664 per the commit (app.js `?v` + version-override `?v` + ANTCV_VERSION seed + sw.js CACHE + TARGET_VERSION, prev `1.51.1644`→STALE). **Standing probes all green on the current `1.51.1683` base (no regression):** PWA suite **1323/1323** (~8.5s), boot-smoke OK (glDemo=function, 0 errors), Personal-panel probe **DIAG PASS** (0 mut/8s, 0 page errors), button-audit **191 buttons / 0 page errors / 0 DEAD / 0 throws / 112 active** (49 not-visible/disabled, 12 skipped-dangerous, 18 ui-only). `app.js` head `(()=>{window` (no `"use strict"`) — minified-sacred intact. **No code shipped by this nightly** (verify-first — the day session owns this lane). > **REGISTER-RECONCILE — STORM-OSCILLATION-GUARD + ALIGN-STORM (antcv-nightly 2026-07-21, Opus 4.8 — no code ship; docs-only reconcile of shipped-but-unregistered day-session fixes).** Baseline moved to PWA `1.51.1644-compl-selflimit` (day sessions shipped 1576→1644 since the 07-19 sweep at 1558; main in sync — fetch+rebase clean). **Standing probes all green on the 1644 base (no regression):** PWA suite **1323/1323** (~28s), boot-smoke OK (glDemo=function, 0 errors), Personal-panel probe **DIAG PASS** (0 mut/8s, 0 page errors), button-audit **190 buttons / 0 page errors / 0 DEAD / 0 throws / 110 active**. `app.js` head `(()=>{window` (no `"use strict"`) — minified-sacred intact; app.js itself was NOT touched by any 1558→1644 commit (all storm work is in the `antcv-sections-normalize-415.js` sidecar + the 3 CJLR alignment sidecars). **Live-verify (PWA layer, curl to pages.dev):** live ANTCV_VERSION seed = TARGET `1.51.1644-compl-selflimit` (**no version regression / no stale-SW mask**); live index.html references `antcv-sections-normalize-415.js?v=1.51.1644-compl-selflimit`; the served sidecar at that `?v` **contains the oscillation guard** (`grep "oscillation held"` = 1) — the P0 freeze fix is deployed + served. Cache-bust quintet fully consistent at 1644 (index.html normalize-415 `?v` + ANTCV_VERSION seed + sw.js CACHE + TARGET_VERSION + version-override own `?v`); STALE_VERSIONS invariant held (prev `1.51.1624-storm-osc-guard` + `1.51.1604-align-storm-001` in STALE, current 1644 only as TARGET). **THE RECONCILE — the P0 "CV preview page-2 oscillation + freeze" fix (owner live-diagnosed 2026-07-19/20) shipped across three legs and is documented NOWHERE in `docs/` (only commit messages + no dedicated unit test), a rule-7 gap; live-served + suite-still-green, now registered:** **(1) ALIGN-STORM-001 (PWA `1.51.1604`, commits 77dec1b/248e3c7):** the visible freeze + certs/interests alignment "jumpiness" was a **text-align write storm** — ~200 style writes/s sustained (measured 5054 text-align mutations + 68 long-tasks over 25s visible). Three CJLR alignment sidecars fought the same cells with DIFFERENT target values: `core-comp-234` observed `attributeFilter:['style']` WHILE `applyPreview` wrote `style.textAlign` on the same node (self-feedback); `antcv-item-align.js`'s body MutationObserver was UNTHROTTLED (full inject+applyAllAlignments sweep on every DOM mutation → throttled to ≥300ms, mirroring section-align's `schedule()` floor); `antcv-section-align.js` wrote `core_comp` cells to a different value than 234 (→ defer `core_comp` to its owner, `ALIGN_OWNED_ELSEWHERE`). This **halved** the storm — the root was upstream. **(2) STORM-OSCILLATION-GUARD-001 (PWA `1.51.1624`, commit 0a020c6):** the upstream root — the `antcv-sections-normalize-415` pipeline ping-pongs with a competing writer. `roleCanonTitles` shortens a role title so `_samePosition` no longer matches its personalInfo source → 415's `repairExperienceCompleteness` thinks the role is "missing" and restores a HIDDEN copy (`[415] experience-completeness restored N missing role(s) hidden`) → `dropCanonHiddenDups`/the competitor strips it right back out → 415 restores again → every cycle dispatches `antcv:sections-updated` → full app re-render → the text-align re-apply storm. Fix (`normalize-415.js:2049`): a time-bounded oscillation guard — refuse to WRITE a serialised `sections` result already written within `__OSC_WINDOW_MS` (~4s); companion STORM-IDEMPOTENT-001 keeps a true no-op silent (write+dispatch only on a REAL `JSON.stringify` diff). Starving 415's dispatch breaks the re-render cascade at its source. A genuine re-visit of an old state (>4s) is unaffected. **(3) STORM-OSCILLATION-GUARD-001 add-side (PWA `1.51.1644`, commit fe3ecdc):** the guard fired (`oscillation held`) but the loop persisted — the competitor produced a slightly-DIFFERENT `sections` each cycle, so 415's serialised result was a moving target the recent-writes dedup missed. Fix (`normalize-415.js:1242`): self-limit `repairExperienceCompleteness` — if it produces the identical missing-role set (signature = `id|title|company`) 3+ times within 6s, suppress the restore (`return null`) so the add side starves and sections converge (nothing to add → nothing for dropCanonHiddenDups/the competitor to strip → storm dies). The restored roles are HIDDEN (`on:false`), a recover-in-one-click safety net, NOT visible content → safe to stop re-adding; a genuine new missing set (real edit/regen) resets the counter and restores normally. **VERIFY-FIRST (this is the day session's active lane — not re-implemented, verified only):** deployed+served confirmed above; both guards are `Date.now()`-window runtime guards installed in the sidecar IIFE observer — no dedicated unit test (suite count unchanged 1323→1323); existing coverage `diag-rerender-storm.mjs` + `unit/sections-normalize-idempotent.test.mjs` don't pin the new windows. **FINDING registered (OPEN_REGISTER, low-pri, NOT actioned — active-lane hygiene):** STORM-GUARD-TEST-COVERAGE-001 — the two P0 freeze guards ship with no dedicated unit test; a future refactor could silently drop the <4s recent-write dedup or the 3-identical-restore self-limit and the freeze would return undetected by CI. Owner/day-session should add a headless `diag-rerender-storm` assertion or a normalize-415 unit test that drives the churn signature. **ALSO undocumented (day-session fixes 1558→1644, rule-7 gaps, NOT registered in detail here — the day session's own lane; flagged for their next reconcile):** MANUAL-SAVE-CATEGORY-001 (`1.51.1576`, 5b41fe9 — manual save uses the real category not hardcoded `unsolicited`), AUTO-COMMIT-FRESHEST-001 (`1.51.1580`, 51c9b77 — commit to the freshest company row not the first), Application-History file-number display (`1.51.1584`, afb5e3b). No code shipped by this nightly. Evidence: OPEN_REGISTER 2026-07-21 STATUS block + `docs/qa/NIGHTLY_2026-07-21_REPORT.md` + FEATURES_REGISTRY (increment 29).

> **JOBTRACKER-PROPOSED-TAB-PATH-QUOTE-001 FIXED + RE-DIAGNOSED (job-tracker nightly 2026-07-19).** The 07-15 finding described this as a "spaces-in-path split" bug in the Proposed-Inbox-tab injection. Investigating tonight (the identical `proposed-tab skipped (...)` message recurred on this run's first `pull --render`) found the REAL cause: `job-tracker-sync.py`'s exception handler did `print(f"proposed-tab skipped ({str(e)[:90]})")` — a 90-char slice that happens to cut a `FileNotFoundError`'s message mid-path, LOOKING like a quoting/split bug but actually just display truncation. The underlying `FileNotFoundError` was real: `JOB_XLSX` (an operator-set env var, not hardcoded) must equal `build_workbook.py`'s hardcoded `OUT` constant — `G:\My Drive\Job Hunt (2026)\Shared material\Job Seek 2026 Nischa\Gabriel_Job_Search_Workbook_2026.xlsx` — exactly, since `build_workbook.py` ignores `JOB_XLSX` entirely and always writes to `OUT`. The scheduled-task SKILL.md only named the containing FOLDER, so every nightly (this one included, first attempt) had to guess the filename, and a folder-name-derived guess (`Job Seek 2026 Nischa.xlsx`) doesn't exist → silent skip, apparently since PROPOSED-INBOX-TAB-001 shipped (the 07-13/07-15 nightlies both hit the identical truncated log line). **Fix:** (1) `scripts/job-tracker/job-tracker-sync.py` now prints `f"proposed-tab skipped ({type(e).__name__}: {e})"` — the full exception, no truncation, so a real future bug isn't masked again; (2) `C:\Users\karpg\.claude\scheduled-tasks\antcv-job-tracker-nightly\SKILL.md` now states the exact `JOB_DOC`/`JOB_BUILD`/`JOB_XLSX` filenames instead of just the folder. **Verified live:** re-ran `pull --render` with the corrected `JOB_XLSX` → `added 'Proposed Inbox' tab (6 leads)` (previously silently skipped every run). No PWA/worker code touched — zero deploy risk. **Also this run:** generated + persisted Teledyne Manufacturing Manager as application_id=2696 (quick tier, `--force` regen since the row's prior artifact pointer, application 805, had been deleted — a variant of JOBTRACKER-STALE-ARTIFACT-POINTER-001 where the target is gone rather than an empty placeholder); verified byte-exact CV 2pg / CL 1pg, 0 banned dashes; doc rev 97→98; Excel refreshed. The gap: the `VERSION` constant (`workers/docx-worker/src/index.js:28758`) was NEVER bumped for LEAD-UNDERLINE-001, so `/health` (+ the `X-AntCV-Worker-Version` header + `_workerVersion` passed to `generateDocx`) all under-reported, and the deploy could not be attested from `/health` alone — it nearly read as "undeployed export leg." **FIX:** bumped `VERSION` → `1.14.161-leadin-underline` (single-occurrence edit, verified), redeployed via `gh workflow run deploy.yml -f target=docx-worker` (run 29465290683 success; CI unit-tests+lint green on Linux); live `/health` now reports `1.14.161-leadin-underline`. **No behavioural change** — the export code was already live; this only makes `/health` truthful and future docx-worker deploys attestable. **DISCIPLINE LESSON (durable):** a docx-worker code change MUST bump `VERSION` in the same commit — the 1.14.x version string is the ONLY `/health`-visible attestation that a worker deploy carried a given change; an un-bumped worker code change is invisible to deploy verification and forces GitHub-Actions log archaeology (as tonight). **SIDE FINDING (not fixed, pre-existing, environmental):** 3 docx-worker diag tests are RED locally on Windows — `diag-pageflow-export.mjs` ("repeatHeader: cont row min shrunk"), `diag-photo-bridge-export.mjs`, `diag-spacing-linkedin-export.mjs` — confirmed IDENTICAL on the unmodified base 65c089b (not caused by this change) and CI unit-tests passed on Linux for both the 1.51.1524 and tonight's deploy, i.e. the known Windows-CRLF-fragile export-geometry diag class (memory `eol-fragile-tests-desktop-vs-cloud`), not a regression. Evidence: OPEN_REGISTER 2026-07-16 staleness-sweep block + FEATURES_REGISTRY increment (25) + `docs/qa/NIGHTLY_2026-07-16_REPORT.md`.

> **JOBTRACKER-STALE-ARTIFACT-POINTER-001 FIXED (data) 2026-07-15 (job-tracker nightly, 2nd dispatch; doc-only PUT, no app/code mutation).** Audit of the tracker doc's `artifacts` map found FOUR `queue=True` high-tier rows pointing at **empty, `category=unsolicited` placeholder apps** instead of their full-fidelity deliverables — so opening any of them from the tracker would show a **blanked JD + unsolicited template + generic 1-page CV**, and because `application_id` was set, `eligible_rows` treated them as "has artifact" and skipped them every run (stuck broken). The correct full-fidelity apps (15 CV / 8 CL sections, real categories, generated tonight ~01:27-01:40) existed but the pointers were never advanced: **demant_pm 667→1019** (product_management), **nvidia_ose 724→1016** (engineering_hardware), **ibsen 796→1017** (program_management — 796 was even a mis-ID'd "Aimpoint/Optical Engineer" record), **aimpoint 904→1018** (engineering_hardware). Root cause: the gen-runner's doc-writeback (or the app-side gen) didn't update `artifacts[uk].application_id` when these rows were re-persisted, leaving the doc pinned to superseded empty apps. **Fix:** rev-safe relay PUT repointing the 4 pointers to the verified-clean apps + clearing every stale `queue=True` ⏰ flag on rows that already have a valid full artifact (doc rev 82→85). Verified: all 23 artifact pointers now resolve to full-fidelity apps (15 CV / 8 CL, correct categories, 0 empty/unsolicited); the 4 targets render byte-exact **CV 2pg / CL 1pg, 0 banned dashes** via `export_pdfs.py`. Excel refreshed rev 85. **NO application records were mutated** (repoint is doc-only → no save-on-open cross-contamination). Owner follow-ups: (1) duplicate app clutter to prune — multiple full-fidelity copies per row (e.g. Demant 807+1019, NVIDIA 808+1016, Ibsen 809+1017, Aimpoint 810+1018) plus stale empty/unsolicited placeholders (667/724/796/904/849/874); (2) the gen-runner should be hardened so a re-persist ALWAYS advances the doc pointer (or the app-side gen writes it back) — otherwise pointer drift recurs. Evidence: OPEN_REGISTER status-advance + this run's chat report.

> **DENSITY FRONTIER + CLEAN-REGEN BATCH 2026-07-15 (owner-driven desktop Opus session; detail in `docs/qa/SESSION_LOG_2026-07-15_DENSITY_REGEN.md` + `docs/qa/DENSITY_SWEEP_REPORT_2026-07-15.md`; memory `layout-density-architecture.md` pts 14-15).** Started as the density-frontier task, expanded live into a full clean-regeneration + deliverables pipeline. **SHIPPED / CLOSED (all on main):** (1) **DENSITY-GROW-VETO-SHRINK-001** (3e31232) — new density_fit lever: after the cross-family auditor vetoes a *grow* as a NEW claim, the retry forces the honest shrink-to-one-line window instead of re-inviting the fabrication; non-regressive (best-state + PUT-on-improvement gating); kill `ANTCV_DENSITY_NO_SHRINK_RETRY=1`; A/B on 810 CV = wash on net quality (honest-by-design, not a silver bullet). (2) **CL-RENDER-HARNESS-STDOUT-001** (3e31232) — `render_payload.mjs` redirects the docx-client's `console.log/info/debug/warn` to stderr; `CL-HYDRATE-EXPORT-GATE-001` was logging `[docx-client]…` to STDOUT and silently blocking 810/849/1006 CLs from measurement/fit (JSONDecodeError). **Durable hazard:** any future docx-client console.log to stdout re-breaks the harness. (4) **GEN-RUNNER-FORCE-001** (303daaa) — `--force` bypasses the `has_art` gate for clean re-gen; active-pointer guard still protects the working app. (5) **DELIVERY-TOOLING-001** (9209bae) — `export_docx.py` (payload→/generate .docx +/generate-pdf, verified) + `analysis_report.py` (LLM fit report per app with EXPANDED gaps + how-to-cover, grounded only in real background — refuses to claim missing credentials, names them honestly with a mitigation). (6) **1007 → `nordic minimal|en` kernel + 14-app clean regen** — 1007 re-compressed 4→2 pages, owner labels preserved, set as the kernel (`PUT /api/kernel-showcase`, verified 15cv/8cl); all 14 queued (`queue:true`) apps regenerated clean with today's fixes (new ids 1016/1017/1018/1019/1021/1025/1026/1027/1028/1031/1075/1120/1173/1222); 28 DOCX + 28 verify-PDF + 14 analysis reports in `Downloads/AntCV_regen_2026-07-15/` (all DOCX OK: 2-page CV, notice present, 0 banned dashes; fit 5 good/5 moderate/4 stretch). **OPEN (documented, NOT shipped — see session log):** (a) **AUTOSAVE-STALE-CLOBBER-001 [HIGH, data-loss]** — opening app 1007 (NO edit) reverted my cloud compression: the app's auto-save/cloud-restore-on-open pushed **stale localStorage `sections` over the newer cloud PUT**. Fix = **server-rev guard on auto-save** (never overwrite a newer cloud rev with older local). Until then, density/cloud writes must not run while the app is open on the account (writes stick once the tab closes). (b) **LINE-DRIFT-MAIN-001** — preview 3pp / export 4pp: the autoPages MAIN column uses per-block `inflate=1` (sidebar gets `SIDEBAR_PREVIEW_INFLATE=1.10`); dense experience under-counts vs the `WORD_INFLATE=1.14` budget → break lands a role late. Fix = per-block export line prediction in `antcv-auto-pagebreak-block-001.js`; needs live-preview calibration (preview-side, headless fixture empty). Distinct from the parallel LINE-DISTRIBUTION-001 (per-row Fit-it). (c) **SIDEBAR-GROUP-PACK-001** — KEY FINDING: the docx-worker renders sidebar groups **in payload order** (proven), so a sidebar group reorder is a CLIENT-SIDE data reorder in `buildPayload`+preview (same helper=parity), **NO worker change**; no-op when tools fits one page (compression fixed 1007 better); parked for a tools-overflow repro + live verify. (d) **JD-MENU-QUEUED-TAB-001** — no "Queued" (alarm-clock/`queue:true`) category/tab in the JD-list UI; feature-add. (e) **KERNEL-COMPRESS-SIDEBAR-GAP-001** — 1007 compressed to 2pp left a 218pt page-2 sidebar balance gap; the kernel was set from this version; `--max-pages 2` may over-compress some of the 14 regens — worth a balance eyeball of the export PDFs.

> **SLOGAN-UNSOL-GENERIC-001 + SLOGAN-CAP-DANGLE-VERB-001 SHIPPED 2026-07-15 (✅ OWNER-CONFIRMED RESOLVED) (owner: an unsolicited application must use the GENERIC standing default slogan, not a role-tailored one; and the truncated "…I BRIDGE" dangling verb; PWA `1.51.1504-slogan-unsol-generic`; diagnosis `docs/qa/SLOGAN-UNSOL-GENERIC-DIAGNOSIS_2026-07-15.md`; memory `slogan-readiness-plan.md`).** Owner report: an unsolicited CL slogan read "AS A PRODUCT AND PROJECT EXPERT, I BRIDGE" — a role-tailored line (wrong for an open application, which has no employer/brand to anchor a "value to THIS employer" slogan) AND truncated mid-clause to a dangling transitive verb. Owner chose option (a): unsolicited keeps the generic specialisation triad. **SHIPPED (three parts, all kill-switchable via `antcv:disable-slogan-unsol-generic`):** (1) **GENERATION** — `scripts/job-tracker/gen-runner.py` no longer persists a tailored `_meta["slogan"]` for an unsolicited row (new `_is_unsolicited(company, category)` mirrors the app's `__ANTCV_UNSOL_RE`; company-variant OR `category=='unsolicited'`); the in-app gen result carve-out blanks `meta.cl_slogan` for unsolicited too (app.src.js `27225` + app.js mirror). (2) **RENDER/EXPORT yields to the generic** — new shared helpers `window.__antcvSloganUnsolActive(io)` + `__antcvSloganOverrideIsGen(ov,io)`; at every slogan read (shared `__antcvResolveSlogan`, the React standalone render, the srcdoc PDF render, and the docx-client DOCX export) an unsolicited app skips `io.cl_slogan` AND an override that merely equals the auto-copied gen slogan → falls through to `io.subtitle` (the Processes • Products • People triad). **A genuinely USER-EDITED override (differs from the gen slogan) is still honored.** The three load-path copies (settings-loader, topbar, override seed) no longer seed a tailored slogan into `meta.cl_slogan`/`antcv:clSlogan` for unsolicited, and `antcv-cl-slogan-fresh.js` `!isTargeted` now drops a gen-equal override so ALREADY-generated unsolicited apps yield WITHOUT a regen. (3) **SLOGAN-CAP-DANGLE-VERB-001 (both caps, defensive)** — `window.__antcvSloganCap` (app.src.js + app.js) gained the Python cap's CLAUSE-cut (first comma/dash/semicolon head at 4..8 words) so "…product and project expert, I bridge strategy…" caps to "As a product and project expert" instead of the dangling "…I bridge"; both caps also drop a trailing `pronoun + verb` fragment (new `__antcvSloganDeDangle` / Python `_scrub_dangle_verb`) so a hard chop never strands a verb. **Verify:** headless assertion (extracted from the shipping app.js) — unsolicited meta resolves to the subtitle triad, an auto-copied override yields, a user-edited override is HONORED, targeted apps unaffected, kill-switch restores old behaviour, and a >8-word slogan with a dangling verb trims to a complete clause (9/9); PWA suite **1313/1313 green** (the 2 GROUP-EMPTY-HIDE mirror-lock failures are fixed upstream); `slogan-meta-stamp` lock test updated to the unsol-gated stamp form. Cache-bust quartet on app.js + antcv-docx-client.js + antcv-cl-slogan-fresh.js. gen-runner.py is Python-only (no cache-bust). PWA auto-deploys from main; docx-worker unchanged (the client blanks the tailored field before it reaches the worker).

> **ROLES CUTOVER + EXPORT-PAGINATION SESSION 2026-07-14→15 (owner live-driving; PWA 1.51.1264→1.51.1265 in this lane; detail in `docs/qa/SESSION_LOG_2026-07-14_CJLR.md` + memory `roles-as-richblock-migration.md`).** Continuation of the CJLR session below. **SHIPPED + LIVE:** (1) **E CUTOVER — ROLES-AS-RICH_BLOCK IS NOW DEFAULT** (`1.51.1264-roles-cutover`, owner "E is approved"): `AntcvRolesRichBlock.isOn()` flipped from opt-in (`flag==='1'`) to default-on (`flag!=='0'`); the flag stays as a `'0'` rollback escape hatch. Verified live with the flag REMOVED: experience renders as rich_block (role heads + role-lines + bullets, editable), NO double-render, stored `roles[]` intact + `items[]` empty + no `__fromRoles` residue → EXPORT unaffected (worker reads roles[]). Chimera dead-code kept as the `'0'` rollback (deletion deferred). (2) **AUTOPAGES-ITEM-TO-ROLE-001** (`1.51.1265-autopages-role`) — the cutover REGRESSED export pagination: under rich_block the experience preview is a FLATTENED items[], so the autoPages measurer keyed page-breaks by ITEM index (13/36/66) while the docx-client payload read them by ROLE index (0..N) → no match → no role.page → the worker never split roles / emitted "(Cont.)", the two columns collapsed to sequential, an 8-page PDF resulted (preview showed 3). Fix: adapter `itemAutoPagesToRoleAutoPages(section, autoR)` maps each item-break to its containing role via the adapter's `item._key` ('roles.R'); docx-client calls it in the experience case gated on `isOn()`. Validated live: `{13:2,36:3,66:4}` → roles `{1:2,4:3,10:4}` → correct (Cont.) headers + parallel columns; export went **8→6 pages**. **EXPORT EVALUATION (6-page PDF):** cont-headers + parallel sidebar restored ✔; the **under-role line now exports for every role** (docx-worker 1.14.157, deployed) — closes the "roles have a line in preview but not export" complaint. **REMAINING (routed to the "Density frontier → 97.5%" session, owner-approved):** (a) preview↔export **line-drift** (real Gabriel unsolicited: preview 3pp / export 4pp — measurer counts fewer lines/para than Word); (b) sidebar **group-swap / page-fill pack** — reorder an overflowing sidebar section's groups so page-1 packs a long+short group (owner: NOT a mid-group split — the coordinator deliberately snaps to group boundaries; must apply in preview AND docx-worker together for parity). **SPAWN STATUS (corrected — "0-ahead worktree" = MERGED to main, NOT stalled):** **Profile per-row CJLR = NOT A BUG** — cycler works (writes `antcvItemAlignment.profile={items.0,0}`, render+item-align apply it); the "doesn't respond" was a **stale service worker** serving pre-1.51.1065 app.js → clear SW, no code change. **Education per-row CJLR** landed (preview `1.51.1424`, worker export `docx-worker 1.14.160`, test-locked). **Worker CJLR export parity** landed (`docx-worker 1.14.156-159`: CJLR parity + group-head scoping + role-bullets-left). **DEPLOY STATUS: all live** — PWA auto-deploys on push (main 1.51.1484); docx-worker 1.14.160 manually deployed (workflow_dispatch `deploy-worker`, 23:19 UTC, after the 1.14.160 commit).

> **META-STICK + LOAD-EDITOR FIXES 2026-07-15 (assistant live-repro session, PWA 1.51.1344-meta-stick-load-editor commit 8a11957 → complete island fix 1.51.1425-tracker-open-claim commit 590ea2e; the two items DEFERRED from the 2026-07-13 PM session below — now BOTH fully RESOLVED).** Both root-caused LIVE (read-only, real cloud state on antcv.pages.dev) then shipped kill-switched; memory `meta-stick-and-load-editor-fixes.md`. **SHIPPED + LIVE-VERIFIED:** (1) **META-STICK-001 (the "Novo ghost") — RESOLVED.** A fresh JD upload cleared the stale rationale + application-questions but NOT the React meta identity `io` (`{company,role,subtitle,greeting,opening}`), so a mislabelled company kept showing and every auto-sync tick STAMPED it onto the row. FIX: both JD-upload handlers now reset `io` to the neutral default right after the existing clear — url-fetch (`Wn`, after `bo(null)`) + file upload (`Hi`, after the `bo(null)` clear). Setter `lo`/minified `Ro` IS in closure scope at both (proven by the pre-existing `bo(null)` clear, declared even later). (Re)gen repopulates it; with io.company empty, auto-sync hits the "skipped" drift branch instead of stamping. Kill: `antcv:disable-jd-meta-reset`. (2) **LOAD-EDITOR-UNSOLICITED-001 — RESOLVED (kernel case).** Job-tracker Open (`prepareAndOpen`) sets the cloud active-pointer + reloads but never claims the app-id for THIS tab, so on reload `getCurrentAppId()==='kernel'` and the cold-restore's raw `__foreignAppId2` drift term fired (`'kernel'!==rowId`), SKIPPING its `else` block (which wraps `lo()`+`ao()` = meta+sections) while the JD-seed block (outside the guard) still ran → "JD loads but editor shows the unsolicited template." Live-proven on real state: active_application id=1006 (cv_sections[15], null pointer stamp) DRIFT_ASIS=true (only `__foreignAppId2`) → DRIFT_AFTER_FIX=false. FIX: a kernel-adopt carve-out in BOTH drift IIFEs (cold-restore + read-from-cloud): a tab with no specific app (`mine==='kernel'`) ADOPTS the pointer's app UNLESS a FOREIGN device set it (cross-device-gen-leak, still covered by the device guard). Aligns the raw check with the tested `AntcvJdScope.shouldAdoptCloudPointer` (kernel→adopt); non-kernel tabs unchanged (PARALLEL-GEN-ISO-001 preserved). Deployed truth table verified live (kernel+realApp=adopt, realAppTab+otherApp=keep, kernel+foreignDevice=keep, killswitch=old). Kill: `antcv:disable-tracker-open-adopt`. **COMPLETE FIX SHIPPED 2026-07-15, PWA island `1.51.1425-tracker-open-claim` (owner-confirmed "ship both"; commit 590ea2e).** The kernel carve-out (1.51.1344) fixed the dominant path; the remaining case — a tracker-open from a tab ALREADY editing a different REAL app (mine=oldRealId≠rowId) — is now closed. New `claimTabAppId(id)` in `src/islands/JobTracker/api.ts` (kill-switched `antcv:disable-tracker-open-adopt`) → `AntcvJdScope.setCurrentAppId(String(id))`, called in `prepareAndOpen` right after `setActive(id)` and BEFORE the namespaced `lastJdText` write (so the JD also lands in the right app namespace). On reload `getCurrentAppId()===rowId` → `__foreignAppId2` false for ANY case → cold-restore restores the saved cv/cl sections. Live-verified: bundle contains the call + kill switch; claim round-trip (setCurrentAppId→sessionStorage→getCurrentAppId, drift=false); boot-smoke clean; pwa suite **1313/1313**. **Co-deployed (stale-bundle repair):** the live island bundle was STALE at `v1.51.619` (last built at commit f42e747) — a rebuild reflects ALL current source, so this also landed the committed-but-never-built **JOBLIST-FILTER-001** (Job List Legend row filter, source 548777d 2026-07-14), the only pending island source change; owner-confirmed to ship both. LESSON: before rebuilding an island, `git log <bundle-last-commit>..HEAD -- src/islands` to see what unbuilt source you'll also ship, and grep the LIVE bundle for a distinctive string to confirm what's actually deployed. **Discipline:** the 1.51.1344 layer was surgical in-place app.js edits mirrored from app.src.js (NO app.js rebuild); the 1.51.1425 layer is the SANCTIONED Vite island build (`vite build` → `pwa/antcv-react-islands.js`), NOT an app.js rebuild. node --check + boot-smoke clean throughout (glDemo=function, 0 errors); boot re-verified live on both deployed bundles. Detail: memory `meta-stick-and-load-editor-fixes.md`.

(2) **CONTACT (1.51.822/1024/1044)** — drop `•` when emoji glyphs present, envelope email icon, em-space separators, font +0.5pt, CV/CL size parity; worker CONTACT-CONVERGE-001 (2-nbsp) converges the DK line. (3) **EDIT-COMMIT-LAG-001 (1.51.1064)** — results/text edits no longer revert on blur; specialisation line gained spellcheck. (4) **SPELL-APPLY-STANDALONE-001 (1.51.1164)** — the preview correction popover only edited the `sections` store, so it silently no-opped on the slogan/specialisation/application line (standalone stores); added `applyStandaloneFix` (pokes the live no-`data-sid` contentEditable text node + fires its own onBlur). **Owner-confirmed working.** Also SPELL-APPLY-COMMIT-001 (native `insertReplacementText` commits immediately) + SPELL-BLIP-NUDGE-001 (net-zero 1px scroll nudge kills the page-fold blip). (5) **SLOGAN Enhance + Fit-it (1.51.1144/1164)** — Enhance = LLM rewrite (4-8 words, undoable) via window-exposed primitives + isolated sidecar logic; **Fit double-cap fix** (`sloganCurrentText` no longer pre-caps → Fit actually trims). **Owner-confirmed: Enhance + Fit work.** (6) **SLOGAN CJLR single-cycler (1.51.1164)** — merged the 4 L/C/R/J buttons into one cycling button; both slogan renders now accept `justify`. (7) **SLOGAN-CJLR-RERENDER-001 (1.51.1204) — the real "CJLR doesn't change in preview" fix:** the `antcv:sections-updated` handler (STORM-IDEMPOTENT-002) only re-renders when the `sections` signature differs, but an align change touches only `localStorage['antcv:clSloganAlign']` → signature matched → no re-render → textAlign never showed.
