> **PHOTO-FLIP-001 — photo horizontal flip (Off/On/Auto), owner live-test session 2026-07-14 (PWA 1.51.761→1.51.842).** New feature: a Flip control in the Settings→Layout collapsible **PROFILE PHOTO** control. **DONE + owner-confirmed:** Off/On mirror the live preview, the exported DOCX/PDF (client canvas-mirrors the PNG), AND the upload-menu carousel avatar; mode/facing live in standalone keys (`antcv:photoFlip`/`antcv:photoFacing`) so **Reset all**/cloud-restore don't wipe them; a detector-version tag (`antcv:photoFacingVer`) re-runs detection on cached photos with no re-upload. **Fixes:** 1.51.842 anchored the control to the position-button row's parent (never the label text — the collapse sidecar caps the label at <40 chars, and label pollution had made the whole panel disappear; a case-INSENSITIVE match had also wrongly attached it to the title-case "Profile Photo" upload block instead of the all-caps Layout control). **AUTO orientation — RESOLVED via gated vision fallback (1.51.942, SHIPPED + verified live).** BlazeFace (1.51.784) reads **head yaw only**; the owner's head-frontal/angled-torso shot needs a *body* cue it can't see, so local detection returned center/unknown. Fix: a new endpoint `POST /api/photo-orientation` in BOTH `workers/proxy` + `workers/demo-proxy` (`photo-orientation.js`, route mirrors jd-analysis; client MODULE D `orientationViaProxy` calls it ONLY when local = center/unknown, once per photo, 12s abort, failure keeps local; `DET_VER=m4` re-runs cached). **Mistral finding (CONFIRMED via raw API response):** the owner asked for Mistral vision, but this account's Mistral key answers even the bare `pixtral-12b` (Mistral's documented vision id) with `served:"ministral-14b-latest"` (HTTP 200, a TEXT model → blind guess) — i.e. **the key has NO Pixtral access** (not a model-id/format bug; owner must enable a Pixtral plan). Handler tries Mistral `pixtral-12b` FIRST but GUARDS it (accepts only a genuinely pixtral-served result, else falls through — so it auto-activates if the key gains Pixtral), and does the real work today via **Claude (claude-sonnet-5, the writer key) → OpenAI (gpt-4o)**, each with its native image-block shape. Deployed both workers (`gh workflow run deploy.yml`); verified live on both: clear left-profile→left, horizontal-flip→right. Full status + design: **`docs/qa/PHOTO-FLIP-001-STATUS.md`**. Memory: `photo-horizontal-flip.md`.

> **RELAY-DETECTION-GAP-001 + ANALYSIS-KERNEL-LOW-TRAFFIC-PIN SHIPPED 2026-07-13 (owner: "pin analysis/kernel bypass until traffic grows; move consensus_poll/long_context to client if no risk; fix the detection gap; production-live"; 1.51.580-detection-gap PWA + access-relay deploy; shift lane 1.51.579-1.51.598; OPEN_REGISTER row 38).** Closes the last of RELAY-TUNE-COVERAGE-GAP-001. **(1) DETECTION-GAP (the keystone):** the cost-quality signals (placeholder_leak/fabrication/banned_word) were BLIND to FORMAT-broken output — a raw SSE frame leaking into the body, an empty body despite billed tokens, control-char garbage, or off-language — so a provider could emit garbage all week and hold health 1.0 (exactly how openai stayed "healthy" through the 3.8.0-3.8.2 SSE-leak bugs), letting the optimizer keep routing to a silently-broken-but-cheap provider. Fix: new pure `detectMalformedOutput(text,ctx)` in `workers/access-relay/src/telemetry.js` (4 break classes; off-language only on a HARD script mismatch, never Latin-vs-Latin) + `malformed_output` quality-signal type + `llm_calls.malformed_output_count` + aggregation into `malformed_output_rate` + a `scoreHealth` penalty (>2% malformed drops a provider out of 'ok' → the client seed demotes it; >20% floors it). Backward-identical at rate 0. Schema + live D1 ALTERs (both tables). Client mirror `pwa/antcv-malformed-detect.js` + one guarded field on the `llm_call` telemetry event (app.src.js + app.js) so every call is tagged. Tests: `malformed-output.test.mjs` 7; relay 105/105. **(2) analysis/kernel LOW-TRAFFIC PIN** (owner "pin but bypass until traffic grows — low number"): `scripts/relay-cost-quality-tune.mjs` `proposeRoles` now judges an UNPINNED role against its BLENDED cost-quality baseline (not a fictional default) and gates the pin behind `activationMinCalls` (default 30). Against real 7d data it correctly BYPASSES analysis (mistral≈blend = no gain; gemini n=11 under floor) and will auto-pin gemini once it clears 30 calls — no production routing changed today. Tests 12→14. **(3) long_context + consensus_poll — NO manual drop (handled by the auto-seed, verified):** the owner's "move to client if no risk" is already delivered by RELAY-COST-TIEBREAK-001 (1.51.578) — its bounded cost penalty puts openai long_context (~0.108 penalty ≥ the 0.10 demotion threshold) and consensus_poll below the cheapest-equal-quality provider, so the client seed auto-demotes the pricey lead each ~5-min cron WHILE keeping it reachable (no voter lost from the consensus vote, fallback intact). A manual `Z.long_context` drop was implemented, tested, then REVERTED — it contradicted that teammate's "no more manual per-task drops needed" design and the seed clears the threshold; the app.src.js comment documents the reasoning. RELAY-DETECTION-GAP-001 is what makes that auto-demotion SAFE (a format-broken cheap provider self-demotes), so the net effect closes Part C without new app.js routing surface. Cache-bust quintet → 1.51.580-detection-gap (malformed sidecar + one guarded llm_call field only); PWA 1257/1257, boot-smoke OK. Isolated worktree.

> **RELAY-COST-TIEBREAK-001 SHIPPED 2026-07-13 (weekly relay cost-quality tune run, owner: "make scoreHealth cost-aware so equal-quality providers tie-break by cost"; 1.51.578-cost-tiebreak; access-relay deployed; OPEN_REGISTER row 38).** Generalizes the manual compress fix (COMPRESS-COST-OPENAI-DROP-001) into a systematic, telemetry-driven mechanism. **Why it needed BOTH sides:** the client health-seed (`__antcvSeedQualityFromD1`) only demoted providers with status degraded/down or health_score <0.6 — it never ordered by health_score among ADEQUATE providers, so a cost nudge to `scoreHealth` alone was INERT. **RELAY (`workers/access-relay/src/telemetry.js`):** `scoreHealth(metrics, costCtx)` now folds a BOUNDED cost penalty into health_score for adequate providers on cost-sensitive tasks — STATUS stays quality-only (a pricey-but-adequate provider is still 'ok', never mislabeled degraded), penalty log-scaled on the cost RATIO vs the task's cheapest-adequate provider, capped 0.15 = the 1.0→0.85 'ok'-floor headroom (cost alone can never cross a status band). `COST_SENSITIVE_TASKS` = {compress, long_context, consensus_poll, consensus_reinforce, fix_orphans, enrich, apply_correction}; quality-critical (generate_cv/cl, parse_jd, analyze_fit) DELIBERATELY excluded (choice stays quality-led). `aggregateHealth` is now two-pass (per-task cheapest-adequate cost → score each provider against it). **CLIENT (`pwa/app.src.js` + minified `app.js` mirror):** the seed now also demotes ADEQUATE cost-losers — a provider whose health_score is ≥0.10 below the task's best adequate (the relay's cost gap) → soft-demote to the BACK of that task's ladder (never removed; failover + adequacy gate still run). Off hot path (seed only), kill switch `localStorage 'antcv:disable-cost-tiebreak'='1'`. **Effect:** long_context + consensus_* (openai-dominated, ~$15/wk) now auto-sink openai behind the cheaper equal-quality provider — same effect the manual Z.compress edit gave compress, now general + refreshed every ~5-min cron. Only a >~20x cost spread makes a demotable gap; 1.5-3x stays a near-tie. **Verification:** NEW `workers/access-relay/tests/cost-tiebreak.test.mjs` 7/7 (no-costCtx backward-identical, cheapest=no-penalty, big-ratio=capped+still-ok, status-gated, clamp-never-below-0.85, monotonic pure fn, task-set membership); PWA suite 1257/1257, boot-smoke OK; app.js/app.src.js node --check clean. Cache-bust quintet 1.51.578-cost-tiebreak. access-relay deployed via deploy.yml (run 29254760225 success, /health 200 ok:true); takes effect next cron aggregation. Shift lane 1.51.578-1.51.597, isolated worktree. ROLLBACK: relay scoreHealth 1-arg quality-only; client remove the __ctbOff block. **v1.1 CORRECTION 2026-07-13 (7af73aa, access-relay redeployed):** production D1 verification exposed that v1 was INERT — it gated the cost penalty on `status === 'ok'`, but the cost-sensitive tasks are inherently high-latency (p95 > 30s) and sit at quality 0.7 ('warning'), so the penalty NEVER fired on the very tasks it targets (all compress/long_context/consensus providers read health 0.7/warning, no cost differentiation). Fix: judge tie-break adequacy on **SUCCESS RATE (≥0.85), not status** — two providers that both succeed are equal-quality enough for cost to decide even when both are latency-'warning'. STATUS stays quality-only (health_score, the client's routing signal, now carries the penalty in the warning band); health clamped ≥0.30 (cost → at worst 'degraded', never 'down'). Pre-pass floor uses the same success criterion. Tests 7→8 (added the warning-band production case + the 0.30 clamp). This is what makes the feature actually reorder routing in prod.

> **OWNER-ROUND-2 GOLD FIXES 2026-07-13 (owner Aimpoint app-810 review, angry that prior fixes did not stick; commit 73264c6; gold-rules 1.5.0).** The owner listed defects I had claimed GREEN that were NOT — root cause: the cascade audit was BLIND to the defect, so it reported false-GREEN. **INTEGRITY FIX (the important one): measure_density._table_cell_scan was rewritten HYPHENATION-ROBUST + COLUMN-ISOLATED.** LibreOffice hyphenates "electro-optical" into "electro-"+"optical" across a line break, which defeated the token-by-token walk, AND other columns/sections interleave at the same y-band. New method: isolate the cell's column x-band, re-band THOSE words into y-lines (interleaving vanishes), accumulate alnum-collapsed segments (ignores hyphen splits) until the label matches; anchors self-validate against the real label geometry (first-row tokens like "optical"/"design" recur elsewhere on the page). Proven: it now flags app 810's 3-line "Optical & electro-optical engineering" that the old scan (and thus the audit) reported clean. **DETERMINISTIC CONTENT FIXES, all control-site-driven so they stick:** (1) SLOGAN-WORD-CAP-001 — CL slogan capped to 9 words (site slogan.max_words); app 810 was 12 words and wrapped; gen-runner._format_slogan._cap_slogan_words caps at generation (clean clause-cut preferred), quality_pass.compress_slogan fixes the 11 stored over-cap slogans (clause-cut or gated LLM-condense); 810 now renders "DEFENCE-GRADE OPTICS FROM LAB TO PRODUCTION" on ONE line. (2) FOCUS-LABEL canonicalization — 11 canonical short forms in gold-rules compressions_labels (kernel FOCUS-LABEL-EO-001: labels ~<=25 chars, <=2 lines); 9 stored apps' long labels compressed ("Optical & electro-optical engineering"->"Electro-optical systems", etc.); 810's cascade proven cleared post-fix. (3) GOOGLE-SCHOLAR markdown-safe — the stored value is "Details available via [Google Scholar](url)"; the old full-string match never fired because of the markdown link; now "Details available via"->"Details via" (drop just "available"), applied to all 20. **DATA:** 20 tracker apps re-passed + re-exported; gold_audit GREEN 20/20 with the CORRECTED detector (trustworthy this time — the detector demonstrably catches the cascade when present). **HONESTLY NOT FIXED (reported, not faked):** (a) CORE-COMP 3-4 ROWS — ALL 20 apps have only 2 data rows; a real competency + its expertise prose cannot be fabricated deterministically, so quality_pass now FLAGS "<3 rows — NEEDS TABLE REGEN"; the golden prompt already mandates 3-4 for future gens; backfilling the 20 needs a grounded generation pass (owner-gated). (b) BRAND COLORS — every app has style_config=null / no meta.styleConfig, so headless export renders the NEUTRAL package palette; per-app brand needs the brand-fit sampling to persist a palette (open brandfit-per-app-scope branch) — NOT guessed (avoids the Teledyne-green mistake). (c) SIGNATURE — signatureB64 is EMPTY in both kernel and cloud prefs (only its align/aspect metadata exists); a signature image cannot be appended because none is stored — needs an actual upload. (d) ROLE-SPLIT "(cont.)" — a multi-bullet role flowing across a page (Sirin Labs) strands bullets with no continuation header; the worker only emits the section CONT header on EXPLICIT role.page assignment (index.js ~27357), not on LibreOffice natural flow — a worker/pagination follow-up (needs deploy). Same-tree note: reconciled a hard multi-session divergence without loss — the concurrent cluster-demand session's local WIP (102c7fb/aad20e4/2ca99b3) preserved on branch cluster-demand-research-writer + preserve/other-session-local; my commit cherry-picked clean onto origin/main.

> **RELAY-TUNE-COVERAGE-GAP-001 SCORER COMPLETED 2026-07-13 (owner ask "fix RELAY-TUNE-COVERAGE-GAP-001"; shift lane 1.51.558-1.51.577; OPEN_REGISTER row 38).** 1.51.538 (f8350a1) fixed the compress lever client-side and extended the router `ROLE_KEYS` to the two proxy cascades (analysis/kernel), and it ADDED `normProvider` + analysis/kernel `ROLE_TASKS` to `scripts/relay-cost-quality-tune.mjs` — but left the loop unable to USE them, so the tune still couldn't score/propose those roles: (1) `normProvider` was defined but NEVER called in `scoreRows` (line stayed `String(r.provider)`), so a `claude` health row didn't fold onto the `anthropic` router id — an anthropic-headed role read as "no data" and could wrongly flip; (2) `proposeRoles` iterated `Object.keys(current)` (MODEL_ROLES = writer/supervisor/coherence only), so the freshly-added analysis/kernel `ROLE_TASKS` were dead — the roles were never scored. **Fix (script-only, no worker source, no MODEL_ROLES change, no deploy):** wire `normProvider` into `scoreRows`; `proposeRoles` now scores the UNION of pinned roles + the router's `TUNABLE_ROLES`, proposing a NEW pin for an unpinned proxy cascade when telemetry warrants (unpinned "keep" stays absent — no MODEL_ROLES noise; min-sample/floor/hysteresis/known-provider guardrails unchanged); coherence maps to its real `apply_correction` label; new `summarizeClientDispatch()` surfaces the CLIENT-dispatched pass-through levers (`compress`/`long_context`/`consensus_*`) that MODEL_ROLES can't reorder (rerouting x-provider 404s) with per-task spend, cheapest-adequate provider, and a QUALITY-GATE caveat (telemetry flags are blind to format-broken output). **Verified vs the real 7-day D1 aggregate (19 health rows):** the loop now sees `analysis` (parse_jd/analyze_fit) + `coherence` (apply_correction), proposes a bounded `analysis` pin (mistral — the only provider clearing min-calls=20; gemini n=11 @53× cheaper is correctly held under-sampled), and prints the client-lever table (`consensus_poll` ~$11/wk + `long_context` ~$5/wk still openai-led → routed to the client `ee()` router as owner decisions). Tests 8→12 (normProvider wiring, coherence label, unpinned-role proposal, client-dispatch surfacing). The loop is now genuinely functional; the remaining items (pin analysis/kernel? move the two other client levers?) are owner-gated non-blocking decisions in row 38.

> **COMPRESS-COST-OPENAI-DROP-001 FIXED 2026-07-13 (weekly relay cost-quality tune run, owner ask: "fix compress"; 1.51.538-compress-openai-drop; OPEN_REGISTER row 38).** The weekly tune surfaced that `compress` (a mechanical CV-tightening pass) burned **openai $62.35 = ~58% of the week's ~$107 LLM spend** (D1 7d: 503 calls @ $0.12395) at 100% success + zero quality flags — vs `gemini` $0.00007 / `mistral` $0.012 / `anthropic` $0.017, all equally adequate. **Root cause:** `compress` provider is chosen CLIENT-side, not by proxy MODEL_ROLES — the dispatcher (`app.src.js` `Z.compress` + `__antcvScoreOrder`/`__LLM_BASE`) scores `openai` HIGHEST for compress (0.23) because `__LLM_BASE` cost proxies are GENERATION-tuned (`openai.c` 0.45, `anthropic.c` 0.90) and INVERT compress's real economics (making the actually-cheap anthropic look priciest). So openai won ~1/4 of compress calls. **Fix (surgical, single authoritative site):** removed `openai` from `Z.compress` (`app.src.js` ~1869 + minified `app.js` mirror) so the real dispatcher's candidate list = `[mistral,gemini,claude]` and the scorer leads with **gemini** (cheapest, 100% success); the full cascade + `fix_orphans` unchanged, anthropic stays reachable. Merely reordering Z is useless (the scorer re-sorts) — removal is required. **~$57-62/wk saved, zero quality loss.** **Verification:** PWA suite 1257/1257, boot-smoke OK (app.js/app.src.js parity gates green). Cache-bust QUINTET 1.51.538-compress-openai-drop (app.js?v + version-override.js?v + sw.js CACHE + version-override TARGET + STALE append 1.51.499/478 + ANTCV_VERSION seed). Shift lane 1.51.538-1.51.557, landed via isolated worktree onto origin/main (f8350a1) after a same-tree race with two parallel sessions — the compress fix + proxy `ROLE_KEYS` groundwork (see below) landed; the scorer companion was ceded to the parallel RELAY-TUNE session. PWA auto-deploys. ROLLBACK: `Z.compress = ["mistral","openai","gemini","claude"]`.

> **MODEL-TABLE-FRESHNESS-001 FIXED 2026-07-13 (desktop shift session, owner ask: "fix tables, and make sure the relay cost quality tune routine is executing the needed modifications"; shift lane 1.51.518-1.51.537, isolated worktree; OPEN_REGISTER row 89).** When AntCV's gen pins moved to `claude-opus-4-8` (flagship, 1.51.332), `gpt-5.4-mini` (default openai) + `gpt-5.5` (thorough tier), and `claude-sonnet-5` (cascade), the two proxy cost tables were left stale — none of `opus-4-8`/`gpt-5.5` had an explicit entry. `demo-enforcement.js` `rateFor()` prices by LONGEST-substring match, so a missing key silently resolves to a shorter neighbour: **`claude-opus-4-8` → legacy `claude-opus-4` [15,75] = 3× OVER-price** (the demo cap burned 3× too fast on every opus-4-8 gen — the exact v1.40.167 bug class the file's own header documents, which had been fixed for 4.5/4.6/4.7 but not the new 4.8), and **`gpt-5.5` → `gpt-5` [1.25,10] = ~24× UNDER-price** (cap burned far too slow). **Fix (4 worker files):** added explicit `'claude-opus-4-8': [5.00, 25.00]` + `'gpt-5.5': [30.00, 60.00]` to `RATES` in BOTH `workers/proxy/src/demo-enforcement.js` and `workers/demo-proxy/src/demo-enforcement.js` (longest-key-wins guarantees the explicit key beats the neighbour), and added `'claude-opus-4-8'` to the `PROVIDER_MODELS.anthropic` fallback cascade in both `multi-llm.js` (the flagship was absent from its own cascade — only mattered on a no-explicit-model cascade call, but wrong). NOT a runtime breakage before the fix (the proxy is pass-through; explicit `model:"claude-opus-4-8"` requests always worked) — a demo-budget-meter accuracy bug. **Verification:** NEW `workers/proxy/test/model-table-freshness.test.mjs` (mirrored to demo-proxy) pins opus-4-8→[5,25], gpt-5.5→[30,60], gpt-5.4-mini→[0.75,4.5], sonnet-5→[3,15], and opus-4-8-in-cascade — 5/5 GREEN; full proxy suite 94/94, demo-proxy 25/25, all 4 files `node --check` clean. **Routine hardening (2nd half of the ask):** `docs/qa/SCHEDULED_ROUTINES.md` RELAY-COST-QUALITY-TUNE-001 gained procedure step **1a MODEL-TABLE FRESHNESS AUDIT** — the weekly tune scores on `cost_per_call`, so a stale price table silently corrupts every routing decision; the routine must now verify the current pins are priced+present (and fix+extend the test in the same run) BEFORE scoring. The routine's scheduled prompt already carries the current model-pins block. No cache-bust (worker-only). Deploy owed: `proxy` + `demo-proxy` via deploy.yml.

Quartet 1.51.389 (also completed .388's half-done bump). **(2) PR #343 resolved:** CONFLICTING because its first commit duplicated 07df2e5 already on main; the unique rescue commit 084aa06 cherry-picked clean onto main (a7cbdfe), suite 1257/1257, PR closed with explanation, branch deleted — register row 85 CLOSED. **(3) PUB-CANONICAL-ORDER-001 (REAL content loss found by the audit):** the worker's PUB-CHAIN-001 renders "TITLE + YEAR" by splitting each citation at the first en-dash/colon — a journal-first citation ("Microsystem Technologies, 2010 – <title>…"; also the "(2010)" paren variant on 806) rendered the JOURNAL as the title and silently DROPPED the real title+authors (hit 808/810/806/804/797/792). quality_pass now reorders to the canonical "Title - Authors, Journal, Year" (no colon/en-dash left → the splitter leaves it whole). **(4) The audit auditing itself:** header-ink contrast was min-glyph-vs-bg only — blind to WHITE ink on a navy fill (the darkest glyph-rect pixel IS the fill) and false-FAILing all 20 apps; now bidirectional (max(glyph_max−bg, bg−glyph_min)). > **JD-REMOVE-STICKY-001 FIXED 2026-07-13 (desktop session, owner repro: "i press on remove … but i refresh and it is added again"; commits f231723 + c90b63e = 1.51.395-jd-remove-sticky; OPEN_REGISTER 2026-07-13 CLOSED block + row 83).** The JD chip's "✕ Remove" cleared only local React state (`Dt(null), Ft(null)`); cloud-restore re-seeded the same `jd_text` from the active application row on every refresh (the OPEN-JD-VISIBLE-001 seeding, which the tracker Open flow NEEDS — the owner's exact concern). **Fix distinguishes the cases with a device-local tombstone:** Remove writes `antcv:jdRemoved` = `<len>:<first-48>` of the removed jd + blanks the `antcv:lastJdText` mirror (cloud `jd_text` untouched — the tracker still owns the row's JD). Cold-restore (occ-1) skips JD-file seeding while `jd_text` matches the tombstone — UNLESS `antcv:lastJdText` equals that jd, which only a tracker Open/Reopen stages right before its reload (`prepareAndOpen` line ~606) → deliberate re-stage, tombstone cleared, seeds normally. The explicit "Read from Cloud" path (occ-2) always clears the tombstone and seeds. A DIFFERENT jd never matches, so uploads/other rows seed normally. app.src.js + surgical minified app.js mirror (4 unique-anchor edits, parse-gated); new `jd-remove-tombstone.test.mjs` runs the predicate from the deployed bundle's own bytes (suppress / re-stage / different-jd). Suite 1257/1257. Shipped through a moving origin (PR #339 merge + night-shift docs landed mid-flight): stash-WIP → rebase → push per the same-tree-commit-race protocol; the pre-push gate caught the version-override `?v` (c90b63e).

> **LO-NESTED-TABLE-DROP-001 FIXED docx-worker 1.14.150 + DIAG-CONVERT-DOCX-001 route 1.14.149 (desktop Fable 5 session 2026-07-13, deployed + live-verified).** The app 723 (ZF zh 1-page CV) knife-edge exports were silently LOSING sections: when content ran a hair past one sheet, CloudConvert/LibreOffice force-split the single body row and — because it collapses the 1-twip separators between section-wrapper tables (PB-WORKER-CONT-HEADER-001) — treated adjacent wrappers as ONE merged table and dropped every wrapper after the first in the run (repro: core_comp + the whole sidebar tail after TOOLS vanished; a 2-char profile diff flipped clean↔lossy; Word rendered the same docx losslessly; the worker never emitted a 2-slot layout — payloads had zero page hints, docx byte-equivalent). Fix in `buildTwoColumnDocument`: `__growSeps` raises each separator whose next sibling is another table to 10 twips (5 measured still lossy) and refunds 9×count from the SIDEBAR cell TOP margin (LO normalizes the row top to the largest cell margin → both columns refund at once; knife-edge true-one-pagers keep their fit). Two refund variants are REJECTED-with-evidence in-code (heading-before sits inside the inter-table gap; cell-bottom shifts the split position — both re-trigger the drop): do not resimplify without re-running the convert matrix. Verified against REAL CloudConvert via the new **POST `/diag/convert-docx`** (raw DOCX body → PDF through the same pipeline/gates as `/generate-pdf`; closes the standing "can't check the LibreOffice render headlessly" gap — local LO 26.2 renders the repro losslessly and CANNOT reproduce): v10/v12/v13/v14 → 1 page complete, v8 → 2 pages complete, FINAL2 CL byte-identical, synthetic hinted 2-slot doc paginates complete; docx diags 38/42 = pre-existing baseline. Bonus: the top-margin refund gave the 723 family enough room that pre-fix "lossy 2-page" variants now genuinely FIT one page. Node-harness gotcha for future diags: importing the worker bundle CLOBBERS `process.argv` — capture args before `import()`.
